- **Callouts**: Sim vs reality — your screen is not the night sky; Pro tip — dim your room
  and screen.
- **Knowledge check**: `single` "A black-hole approach makes you feel… → **higher than
  you are, so you fly too low**".
- **Go deeper**: `faa-pilot-vision`, `phak-aeromedical`.

#### L12.2 — Aircraft and airport lighting (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L12.1 · **Widgets** W27 · **Challenges** C12.2
- **Objectives**: identify runway, taxiway, approach and threshold lights; read a PAPI and
  a VASI; activate pilot-controlled lighting.
- **Outline**: aircraft lights and when to use them (91.209); airport beacon colours;
  runway and taxiway lighting colours (W27); PAPI and VASI; approach lighting systems;
  pilot-controlled lighting (7/5/3 clicks).
- **Callouts**: Sim vs reality — PCL support (Section 9.2).
- **Knowledge check**: W27 hotspots; `single` PAPI "two white two red" → **on path**.
- **Practice**: `light-signals` (lighting cards). **Go deeper**: `aim-ch2-lighting`.

#### L12.3 — Night operations: preflight to landing (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L12.2 · **Widgets** W27, W18 · **Challenges** C12.1
- **Objectives**: complete a night preflight and taxi; fly a night pattern with the
  correct lighting; land with and without the landing light.
- **Outline**: flashlight and cockpit lighting; taxi at night (lights, slow, signs);
  takeoff and the "black" climb-out — trust the instruments; the night pattern (wider,
  higher, earlier checklist); the landing light and the flare; night currency
  (Section 10.4) — and why it exists.
- **Callouts**: Safety — somatogravic illusion on takeoff; Classic panel — panel lighting.
- **Knowledge check**: `single` night currency rule; `multi` night pattern changes.
- **Go deeper**: `afh-night`.

#### L12.4 — Night cross-country and night emergencies (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L12.3, L6.6 · **Widgets** W15 · **Challenges** C12.3,
  C12.4
- **Objectives**: plan a night cross-country with lit checkpoints and a 45-min reserve;
  handle an electrical failure or engine failure at night.
- **Outline**: route choice (lit areas, airports along the way, terrain); checkpoints at
  night (cities, beacons, highways); altitude and terrain; the dark-area engine failure
  (glide toward lights, W15); electrical failure at night (flashlight, load shedding —
  links to L14.1).
- **Callouts**: Decision — "Would you cross this dark area at this altitude?"
- **Knowledge check**: `numeric` reserve fuel; `single` best checkpoint at night.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `afh-night`, `aopa-night`.

### Module 13 — Mountain flying

#### L13.1 — Mountain weather and airflow (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L11.1 · **Widgets** W29 (P1) · **Challenges** C13.4 (P1)
- **Objectives**: describe windward lift and lee-side sink; recognise the signs of
  mountain wave; set a wind limit for a crossing.
- **Outline**: why wind accelerates over ridges; lee-side downdrafts and rotors (W29);
  lenticular clouds; winds aloft at ridge level; afternoon convection; the Sierra in
  summer.
- **Callouts**: Safety — the "In a real airplane" box (Section 13.8); Decision — wind
  limit.
- **Knowledge check**: `single` "Crossing a ridge with a strong wind, expect sink on…
  → **the lee side**".
- **Go deeper**: `faa-tips-mountain-flying`, `aim-ch7`.

#### L13.2 — Planning a mountain route (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L13.1, L6.6 · **Widgets** W21 · **Challenges** C13.2
- **Objectives**: choose a route along roads and passes with escape options; plan ridge
  crossing altitudes and angles; identify emergency landing options.
- **Outline**: follow the passes (I-80 over Donner Summit); crossing altitude and the 45°
  approach to ridges; escape routes and "the way out is behind you"; emergency options
  (KBLU, valleys, roads — last resort); fuel and daylight margins; the Sierra route
  worksheet (kneeboard).
- **Knowledge check**: `single` ridge crossing angle → **45°**; `numeric` crossing altitude.
- **Go deeper**: `faa-tips-mountain-flying`, `skyvector`.

#### L13.3 — High-elevation airport operations (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L13.2, L9.2 · **Widgets** W21, W22 · **Challenges**
  C13.1, C13.3
- **Objectives**: lean for takeoff at a high airport; compute takeoff distance and climb
  rate at high DA; fly an approach with a higher groundspeed.
- **Outline**: leaning for takeoff (⚠ verify method); abort point ("70% of lift-off speed
  by 50% of the runway" rule of thumb, ⚠ verify); climb gradient vs terrain; approach
  with high TAS; the go-around at altitude.
- **Callouts**: Decision — abort point; Sim vs reality — engine power model at altitude.
- **Knowledge check**: `numeric` W22 question at 6,000 ft DA; `single` abort point rule.
- **Go deeper**: `faa-tips-mountain-flying`, `phak-performance`.

#### L13.4 — Canyon turns and escape routes (P1)

- **Est.** 10 min · **Prereqs** L13.2
- **Objectives**: explain why you should never enter a canyon without room to turn;
  describe an escape turn (awareness only — no challenge).
- **Go deeper**: `faa-tips-mountain-flying`.

### Module 14 — Systems failures and advanced emergencies

#### L14.1 — Electrical failures (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L1.3 · **Widgets** W30 · **Challenges** C14.1
- **Objectives**: recognise an alternator failure from the EIS and annunciations; shed
  electrical load; decide where to land before the battery runs out.
- **Outline**: the 172S electrical system (W30); what alternator failure looks like; the
  checklist flow (in our own words, Appendix B.4); load shedding; battery time and the
  standby battery; landing as soon as practical.
- **Callouts**: Sim vs reality — battery endurance in the sim (Week 6 notes).
- **Knowledge check**: `single` ammeter/load reading after alternator failure; `order`
  the flow.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `phak-systems`.

#### L14.2 — Instrument and avionics failures (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L1.2 · **Widgets** W31 · **Challenges** C14.2, C14.3
- **Objectives**: predict ASI, altimeter and VSI behaviour for pitot and static
  blockages; use the alternate static source; fly with a failed PFD in reversionary mode.
- **Outline**: pitot-static system (W31); pitot blockage (ram and drain), static blockage;
  alternate static; AHRS/ADC failures on the G1000 (red X); reversionary mode; flying
  pitch and power when the airspeed lies.
- **Callouts**: Classic panel — vacuum-driven gyros (if the variant has them).
- **Knowledge check**: `single` for each blockage type (W31 quiz mode); `single` where the
  DISPLAY BACKUP button is.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `phak-instruments`, `ifh-instruments`.

#### L14.3 — Partial power loss and fuel problems (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L5.4 · **Widgets** W15 · **Challenges** C14.4
- **Objectives**: troubleshoot a rough engine (fuel, mixture, magnetos, aux pump);
  decide between continuing, landing nearby and a forced landing.
- **Outline**: partial power loss is often harder than a complete failure; the
  troubleshooting flow (Appendix B.5); fuel starvation vs exhaustion; flying to the
  nearest airport with W15 range; when to stop troubleshooting and land.
- **Knowledge check**: `order` troubleshooting steps; scenario `single`.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `afh-emergency`.

#### L14.4 — Fire, smoke and emergency descent (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L14.3 · **Challenges** C14.5
- **Objectives**: fly the engine-fire memory items; fly an emergency descent within
  limits; handle an electrical fire and smoke in the cabin.
- **Outline**: engine fire in flight (Appendix B.6, ⚠ verify against the in-sim
  checklist); electrical fire and smoke; emergency descent technique (⚠ verify speed,
  bank and flap guidance); the forced landing that follows.
- **Callouts**: Sim vs reality — the sim may not model fire; the event card replaces it.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `afh-emergency`.

#### L14.5 — Off-airport landings and ditching (P1)

- **Est.** 15 min · **Prereqs** L14.3
- **Objectives**: choose a field by surface, slope and length; describe ditching technique
  (awareness).
- **Go deeper**: `afh-emergency`.

### Module 15 — Advanced airspace, ATC and online flying

#### L15.1 — Class B and Class C operations (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L6.2, L7.3 · **Widgets** W11 · **Challenges** C15.1
- **Objectives**: request and receive a Class B clearance; enter Class C correctly; use
  VFR flyways and transition routes.
- **Outline**: the rules (91.130, 91.131, ⚠ verify); "cleared into the Class Bravo" vs
  "remain clear"; Class C: two-way communication and what counts; the Mode C veil; SFO's
  shelves on W11; VFR flyways and published transitions (⚠ verify any Bay Area route).
- **Knowledge check**: `single` which phrase allows Class B entry → **"Cleared into the
  Class Bravo airspace"**; `single` Class C entry rule.
- **Practice**: `airspace-and-minimums`, `radio-phraseology`. **Go deeper**: `aim-ch3`,
  `faa-tac`.

#### L15.2 — Flight following and working approach control (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L7.4 · **Challenges** C15.2
- **Objectives**: request flight following; respond to traffic calls, altitude and
  heading assignments; handle handoffs and termination.
- **Outline**: the request (who, where, what, request); traffic calls and "traffic in
  sight"/"looking"; frequency changes; "radar service terminated, squawk VFR".
- **Practice**: `radio-phraseology`. **Go deeper**: `aim-ch4`.

#### L15.3 — Light gun signals, lost comms and special VFR (P0)

- **Est.** 15 min · **Prereqs** L7.3 · **Challenges** C15.4 (P1)
- **Objectives**: interpret every light gun signal on the ground and in the air; follow
  the VFR lost-comms procedure at a towered airport; state the special VFR rules.
- **Outline**: light gun table (AIM, Appendix B.9); lost comms VFR (7600, remain VFR,
  enter the pattern, watch the tower); special VFR (91.157, ⚠ verify).
- **Practice**: `light-signals`. **Go deeper**: `aim-ch4`.

#### L15.4 — Readbacks and phraseology (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L7.1 · **Widgets** W32, W19 · **Challenges** C15.3
- **Objectives**: read back every required element of an instruction; use standard
  phraseology for requests; recover from a missed call ("say again").
- **Outline**: what must be read back (hold short, runway, altitude, heading, frequency,
  squawk, clearance); the structure of a good call; W32 practice; common sim-ATC
  differences.
- **Practice**: `radio-phraseology`. **Go deeper**: `aim-ch4`, `pilot-controller-glossary`.

#### L15.5 — Flying on VATSIM (P0)

- **Est.** 30 min · **Prereqs** L15.4 · **Challenges** C15.5 (P1)
- **Objectives**: create an account and meet the network's requirements; configure a
  pilot client for MSFS 2024; file a VFR flight plan and make a first flight following
  the Code of Conduct.
- **Outline**: what VATSIM is; account, pilot training and ratings (⚠ verify the current
  requirements); installing and configuring the pilot client (⚠ verify); VFR flight plans
  and remarks (e.g. "new member"); UNICOM when no controller is online (⚠ verify
  frequency and practice); listening first; etiquette; vZOA resources for the Bay Area.
- **Callouts**: Note — Learn-To-Fly is not affiliated with VATSIM (Section 54.2).
- **Go deeper**: `vatsim`, `vatsim-code-of-conduct`, `vzoa`.

### Module 16 — Advanced VFR capstone

#### L16.1 — Risk management for longer flights (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** Modules 9–15
- **Objectives**: apply the 5P check at each phase of flight; use a personal minimums
  worksheet; recognise hazardous attitudes.
- **Outline**: 5P (Plan, Plane, Pilot, Passengers, Programming); personal minimums
  worksheet (kneeboard); the five hazardous attitudes and antidotes; fatigue on long
  flights.
- **Go deeper**: `risk-management-handbook`.

#### L16.2 — Preparing for the Advanced VFR stage check (P0)

- **Est.** 15 min · **Prereqs** Modules 9–15 · **Challenges** C16.1, C16.2
- **Objectives**: describe what the stage check covers and the standard for each task;
  plan the capstone cross-country.
- **Outline**: stage check format; self-check list with review links; the capstone route
  and planning worksheet.

### Module 17 — Attitude instrument flying

#### L17.1 — The instrument scan (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** Course 1 (Course 2 recommended) · **Widgets** W33 ·
  **Challenges** C17.1
- **Objectives**
  1. Name the control and performance instruments on the G1000 PFD.
  2. Describe a radial scan and the three common scan errors.
  3. Set a known attitude and power for each phase of flight.
- **Outline**
  1. Why your inner ear lies (preview of L17.5).
  2. Control and performance (Section 11.1) with W33.
  3. The radial scan; fixation, omission, emphasis.
  4. The Skyhawk's attitude-and-power table for IMC (from `aircraft.yaml`, ⚠ verify).
  5. Trim, trim, trim.
  6. Setting up the sim for instrument practice (Section 9.5).
- **Callouts**: In a real airplane — view-limiting devices and a safety pilot; Pro tip —
  "Attitude + power = performance".
- **Knowledge check**: W33 quiz ("Which instrument shows the problem?"); `single` scan
  error definitions.
- **Practice**: `limits-and-speeds` (IMC attitudes and power). **Go deeper**:
  `ifh-attitude-instrument-flying`.

#### L17.2 — Basic instrument manoeuvres and patterns (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L17.1 · **Widgets** W33 · **Challenges** C17.1, C17.2,
  C17.5 (P1)
- **Objectives**: fly straight-and-level, standard-rate turns, constant-airspeed and
  constant-rate climbs and descents to Gold tolerances; fly timed turns; fly Pattern A.
- **Outline**: level flight; standard-rate turns and the rate-of-turn indicator; climbs
  and descents (constant airspeed vs constant rate); level-off leads; timed turns (15 s
  per 45°); Pattern A (diagram and timing, ⚠ verify the version taught).
- **Knowledge check**: `numeric` standard-rate bank at 100 KTAS (→ **17°** ±2);
  `numeric` timed turn seconds for 90° (→ **30**).
- **Go deeper**: `ifh-attitude-instrument-flying`.

#### L17.3 — Unusual attitude recovery (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L17.2 · **Widgets** W34 · **Challenges** C17.3
- **Objectives**: identify nose-high and nose-low unusual attitudes from the PFD and the
  standby instruments; recover in the correct order for each.
- **Outline**: how unusual attitudes happen (distraction, disorientation, turbulence);
  the G1000's extreme-attitude declutter (⚠ verify behaviour in the sim); nose-high
  recovery (power, nose down, wings level); nose-low recovery (power off, wings level, ease
  out of the dive) — Appendix B.10; W34 drills.
- **Knowledge check**: W34 quiz; `order` nose-low recovery.
- **Practice**: `memory-items` (recoveries). **Go deeper**: `ifh-attitude-instrument-flying`,
  `afh-upset`.

#### L17.4 — Partial panel and the magnetic compass (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L17.2 · **Widgets** W35 (P1) · **Challenges** C17.4
- **Objectives**: fly on the standby instruments after an AHRS or PFD failure; make timed
  turns and compass turns to headings; describe acceleration and turning errors.
- **Outline**: what "partial panel" means in a G1000 airplane (standby attitude,
  airspeed, altimeter, compass); timed turns without a heading indicator; compass errors
  (ANDS, UNOS) and the latitude rule (Appendix F.7, W35); getting help from ATC ("no-gyro
  vectors").
- **Knowledge check**: `single` ANDS; `numeric` compass-turn lead from north at 38° N with
  15° bank.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `ifh-attitude-instrument-flying`,
  `phak-instruments`.

#### L17.5 — Spatial disorientation (P0)

- **Est.** 15 min · **Prereqs** L17.1
- **Objectives**: explain how the vestibular system causes the "leans", graveyard spiral
  and somatogravic illusions; state the only cure (trust the instruments).
- **Outline**: the inner ear; illusions (leans, Coriolis, graveyard spiral, somatogravic,
  inversion); why the sim can't make you feel them — and why that's dangerous if you fly
  for real.
- **Callouts**: In a real airplane — the box in Section 13.8.
- **Go deeper**: `faa-spatial-disorientation`, `ifh-human-factors`.

### Module 18 — The IFR system

#### L18.1 — IFR rules: minimums, currency, alternates and fuel (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L11.2
- **Objectives**: decide whether an alternate is required; compute IFR fuel; state IFR
  currency and when you need a clearance.
- **Outline**: VFR vs IFR and when IFR is required; the rules table (Section 11.2);
  the 1-2-3 rule with worked TAFs; alternate minimums and the "A" symbol on plates; fuel
  requirements; instrument currency and proficiency.
- **Knowledge check**: scenario `single` on the 1-2-3 rule (×2); `numeric` IFR fuel.
- **Practice**: `ifr-rules`. **Go deeper**: `ecfr-part-91`, `ifh-ifr-flight`.

#### L18.2 — IFR charts and the approach plate (P0)

- **Est.** 30 min · **Prereqs** L18.1 · **Widgets** W38
- **Objectives**: read an enroute low chart (airways, MEA, MOCA, fixes); read every part
  of an approach plate (briefing strip, plan view, profile, minima, airport sketch).
- **Outline**: enroute low charts (annotated example); the approach plate top to bottom
  with W38 (the original training plate, D2-10); minima lines and categories; notes and
  symbols; where to download current plates (d-TPP) and why the cycle matters.
- **Callouts**: Safety — never use an out-of-date plate for real flight.
- **Knowledge check**: W38 hotspot questions (×3); `single` MEA vs MOCA.
- **Practice**: `approach-plate-symbols`, `chart-symbols`. **Go deeper**: `faa-dtpp`,
  `faa-chart-users-guide`.

#### L18.3 — Clearances and departures (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L18.2 · **Widgets** W32 · **Challenges** C18.1
- **Objectives**: copy and read back a clearance using CRAFT; brief a departure
  procedure and takeoff minimums; describe void times and "released".
- **Outline**: how clearances are delivered (clearance delivery, ground, FSS/phone at
  non-towered fields); CRAFT shorthand; W32 in clearance mode; ODPs and takeoff minimums
  (KLVK example, ⚠ verify); climb gradients.
- **Practice**: `ifr-rules`, `radio-phraseology`. **Go deeper**: `aim-ch5`, `iph-departures`.

#### L18.4 — The G1000 NXi and GFC 700 for IFR (P0)

- **Est.** 30 min · **Prereqs** L6.5, L6.8 · **Widgets** W2, W36 · **Challenges** C18.2
- **Objectives**: build a flight plan with a departure, airways (if available) and an
  approach; select the CDI source and understand GPS CDI scaling; use the autopilot modes
  for climbs, enroute and approaches.
- **Outline**: FPL page and procedure loading (PROC key); activating legs and
  vectors-to-final; CDI source and scaling (W36, Appendix F.6); autopilot modes table
  (Section 8.5) and the mode annunciator; "What's it doing now?" — automation surprises;
  the before-approach setup.
- **Callouts**: Sim vs reality — Working Title G1000 differences (⚠ verify).
- **Knowledge check**: `single` "Armed modes are shown in… → **white**" (⚠ verify
  colour); `order` loading an approach.
- **Go deeper**: `garmin-g1000nxi-guide`.

#### L18.5 — Enroute IFR and lost communications (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L18.4 · **Challenges** C18.3
- **Objectives**: fly an airway with correct altitudes; make required reports; apply the
  lost-comms rules for route and altitude.
- **Outline**: airways and direct routing with GPS; altitudes (MEA, MOCA, cruising
  altitudes for IFR, ⚠ verify); required reports in a radar environment; lost comms
  (AVEF, MEA/expected/assigned — Section 11.2) with a worked example.
- **Practice**: `ifr-rules`. **Go deeper**: `iph-enroute`, `ecfr-91-185`.

### Module 19 — Holding

#### L19.1 — Holding patterns: entries, timing and wind (P0)

- **Est.** 30 min · **Prereqs** L18.5, L6.4 · **Widgets** W37 · **Challenges** C19.1
- **Objectives**: visualise a hold from an ATC instruction; choose the correct entry; fly
  the hold with timing and wind correction.
- **Outline**: why holds exist; the holding instruction (direction, fix, radial/course,
  legs, EFC); drawing the hold on the HSI; entries with W37 (Appendix F.5); timing and
  wind (Section 11.3); the 5 Ts; speed limits (⚠ verify).
- **Knowledge check**: W37 quiz (×4 entries); `numeric` outbound time correction.
- **Practice**: `holding-entries` (dynamic deck from W37). **Go deeper**: `aim-ch5-holding`,
  `iph-holding`.

#### L19.2 — Holding with the G1000 and autopilot (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L19.1 · **Widgets** W37 · **Challenges** C19.2
- **Objectives**: create a hold in the G1000 at a fix; let the GPS sequence the entry;
  monitor it on the autopilot and exit when cleared.
- **Outline**: published holds and holds-in-lieu of procedure turns; user holds (⚠ verify
  G1000 NXi steps in the sim); suspend (SUSP) and how to exit; monitoring the automation.
- **Go deeper**: `garmin-g1000nxi-guide`.

### Module 20 — Instrument approaches

#### L20.1 — Anatomy of an instrument approach (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L18.2 · **Widgets** W38
- **Objectives**: name each approach segment and fix; read minima and choose the correct
  line; brief an approach using a standard briefing format.
- **Outline**: segments (IAF, IF, FAF, MAP) on W38; vectors-to-final vs full procedure;
  minima: DA vs MDA, visibility, category A; the approach briefing (Appendix B.12);
  stabilised approach criteria for IFR.
- **Practice**: `approach-plate-symbols`. **Go deeper**: `iph-approaches`.

#### L20.2 — RNAV (GPS) approaches (P0)

- **Est.** 30 min · **Prereqs** L20.1, L18.4 · **Widgets** W36, W39 (P1) · **Challenges**
  C20.1
- **Objectives**: load and activate an RNAV (GPS) approach; fly an LPV or LNAV/VNAV to DA
  and an LNAV to MDA; confirm the approach mode annunciation before the FAF.
- **Outline**: WAAS, LPV, LNAV/VNAV, LNAV (Section 11.4); the annunciations that must be
  checked (⚠ verify G1000 NXi wording); T-shaped approaches and hold-in-lieu; flying the
  glidepath (W36); LNAV with CDFA and advisory glidepaths (W39).
- **Go deeper**: `aim-ch1-gps`, `iph-approaches`.

#### L20.3 — ILS approaches (P0)

- **Est.** 30 min · **Prereqs** L20.1 · **Widgets** W36 · **Challenges** C20.2, C20.3
- **Objectives**: tune, identify and set up an ILS; intercept the localizer and
  glideslope; fly to DA hand-flown and coupled.
- **Outline**: localizer and glideslope geometry and sensitivity (W36, Appendix F.6);
  identifying the ILS (Morse); the CDI source switch from GPS to LOC (⚠ verify automatic
  switching in the G1000); intercept angles; glideslope intercept from below; decision
  altitude and the runway environment; coupled approach with APR mode.
- **Knowledge check**: W36 quiz; `single` "the ILS needle is four times more sensitive
  near the runway because… → **the course narrows (angular)**".
- **Go deeper**: `iph-approaches`, `ifh-navigation`.

#### L20.4 — VOR and other non-precision approaches (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L20.1, L6.4 · **Widgets** W36, W39 (P1) · **Challenges**
  C20.4
- **Objectives**: fly a VOR approach to MDA; use CDFA or step-downs; identify the missed
  approach point by distance or timing.
- **Outline**: VOR and LOC approaches; step-down fixes; CDFA vs dive-and-drive (W39); VDP
  and when you can leave MDA (91.175); timing from the FAF.
- **Go deeper**: `iph-approaches`.

#### L20.5 — Missed approaches (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L20.2 · **Widgets** W38 · **Challenges** C20.5, C20.6
  (P1)
- **Objectives**: execute a missed approach at DA or the MAP; fly the published missed
  approach to the holding fix; handle a missed approach on the autopilot (GA and
  sequencing).
- **Outline**: when to go missed (including "not stabilised"); the flow (power, pitch,
  positive rate, flaps, navigate, talk — Appendix B.13); the published missed approach on
  W38; the G1000's missed-approach sequencing (⚠ verify); what to do next (hold, try
  again, divert).
- **Practice**: `memory-items`. **Go deeper**: `iph-approaches`.

#### L20.6 — Circling approaches and procedure turns (P1)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** L20.4
- **Objectives**: describe circling minima and protected areas; fly a procedure turn and
  a hold-in-lieu (awareness).
- **Go deeper**: `iph-approaches`.

### Module 21 — Instrument capstone

#### L21.1 — Single-pilot IFR and automation management (P0)

- **Est.** 20 min · **Prereqs** Modules 17–20 · **Challenges** C21.2
- **Objectives**: plan workload by phase of flight; choose the right level of automation;
  set personal IFR minimums.
- **Outline**: workload peaks; briefing early; levels of automation; personal IFR
  minimums worksheet; the IFR cross-country plan (Section 11.5).
- **Go deeper**: `risk-management-handbook`, `ifh-ifr-flight`.

#### L21.2 — IFR emergencies (P0)

- **Est.** 25 min · **Prereqs** L21.1, L14.1, L14.2 · **Challenges** C21.1, C21.3 (P1)
- **Objectives**: handle an alternator failure in IMC; fly an approach on partial panel;
  apply lost-comms rules end to end.
- **Outline**: declaring an emergency and asking for help; electrical failure in IMC
  (land soon, shed load, battery time); partial panel in IMC (no-gyro vectors, the
  simplest approach available); lost comms flight end to end.
- **Practice**: `memory-items`, `ifr-rules`. **Go deeper**: `ifh-emergencies`.

---

## 16. Challenge specifications (every v2 challenge)

### 16.1 Shared conventions (additions to v1 Section 15.1)

**New challenge types:** `planning` (done on the ground, in the browser), `drill`
(auto-graded in a widget), `night`, `instrument`, `approach`, `holding`, `stage-check`.
v1 types stay. A challenge has one primary type and optional `tags` (e.g. `night`,
`ifr`, `mountain`, `failure`) used for filters.

**New start states**

| Name            | Meaning in MSFS 2024                                                                                                                |
| --------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `IFR_RAMP`      | `RAMP_RUNNING` with the IFR flight plan from the brief loaded in the G1000 (the brief lists every waypoint and the procedure names) |
| `AIR_START_IMC` | `AIR_START` inside or above a cloud layer. Fallback: `RUNWAY` start and climb into the layer; timer starts at the start point       |

**New weather presets** (added to `content/presets.yaml`; ⚠ verify each can be built in
the sim and behaves as described — Section 9.3)

| Name               | Settings                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `WX_HOT`           | KLVK area: clear, wind 270° at 8 kt, **38 °C**, altimeter 29.82                                                                                 |
| `WX_HOT_SIERRA`    | Sierra: clear, wind 240° at 10 kt, **30 °C at Truckee**, altimeter 30.05                                                                        |
| `WX_GUST_XW`       | Variant set: crosswind 8 kt, 12 kt, 12G18, 15G22 at 60–90° to the runway (the variant picks one)                                                |
| `WX_MARINE_LAYER`  | Coast: overcast 1,200 ft lowering to 600 ft over 20 min (⚠ can the sim animate this? Fallback: two variants, 1,200 ft and 600 ft); inland clear |
| `WX_CLOUD_BANK`    | Scattered at 4,000 ft becoming broken 3,500 ft ahead of the aircraft; tops 7,000 ft                                                             |
| `WX_NIGHT_CLEAR`   | As `WX_CALM`; used with a night time preset                                                                                                     |
| `WX_MOUNTAIN_CALM` | Sierra: clear, winds aloft 240° at 10 kt at 12,000 ft                                                                                           |
| `WX_MOUNTAIN_WAVE` | Sierra: winds aloft 250° at 35 kt (10,000 ft) and 45 kt (14,000 ft), few lenticular clouds if the sim supports them                             |
| `WX_IMC_HIGH`      | Overcast 1,500 ft AGL, tops 6,000 ft, visibility 5 sm, light winds — enroute and holding                                                        |
| `WX_IMC_NP`        | Overcast 700 ft AGL, visibility 2 sm, wind 250° at 8 kt — non-precision minimums                                                                |
| `WX_IMC_LOW`       | Overcast 300 ft AGL, visibility 1 sm, wind 250° at 6 kt — precision minimums                                                                    |
| `WX_IMC_BELOW`     | Overcast 150 ft, visibility ½ sm — **below minimums**: the missed approach is the only right answer                                             |
| `WX_IMC_CIRCLE`    | Broken 1,000 ft, visibility 3 sm, wind favouring the opposite runway                                                                            |

**New time presets:** `TIME_DAY` (10:00, late spring — v1 default),
`TIME_HOT_AFTERNOON` (15:00, early August), `TIME_NIGHT` (21:30 local, mid-October, quarter
moon), `TIME_NIGHT_DARK` (same with a new moon — ⚠ verify the sim models moon phase and
pick real dates), `TIME_DAWN` (06:30, for mountain departures). Presets carry a
`night: true|false` flag the logbook uses.

**New load presets:** `LOAD_FULL` (v1 P1 → P0), `LOAD_FOUR` (four adults 170 lb each, 30
gal), `LOAD_AFT_CG` (pilot plus 120 lb in baggage area 1, 40 gal — within limits but
near the aft limit; ⚠ verify with W23).

**Scenario variants.** A challenge may define 2–6 `variants`. Each variant overrides
only setup fields (weather, runway, time, the failure in an event, the go/no-go data set)
and optionally tier _descriptions_; it never changes criterion ids, kinds, weights or
required flags, so attempts stay comparable. The Brief shows a **"Roll a scenario"**
button (or the learner picks one). The chosen `variantId` is saved with the attempt, and
the history shows which variants have been passed.

**Events** (replaces v1's `randomEvents`, which remain valid as `prompt` events):

| Kind       | What the Fly tab does                                                                                                 | Example                                   |
| ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- |
| `prompt`   | Sound + large card with a message                                                                                     | "Go around!" (v1 C8.1)                    |
| `failure`  | Sound + failure card: what failed, the instructor-style action to simulate it, and the optional sim-menu path (D2-09) | "Alternator failure: switch ALT off now." |
| `lookAway` | Timed instruction for unusual attitudes: "Look away and follow these inputs until the beep"                           | C17.3                                     |
| `atc`      | A scripted ATC instruction for self-voiced mode, with an optional speech-synthesis read-out (P1)                      | "Hold southeast of ECA…"                  |

Events have `minSeconds`/`maxSeconds` (random time window), optional `chance`, and may
be restricted to a leg. Timestamps are saved with the attempt (as v1).

**Legs.** Long challenges can be split into `legs` (e.g. "Departure", "Pass crossing",
"Arrival"). Criteria can belong to a leg; the Fly tab shows one leg at a time and the
debrief groups criteria by leg. Scoring is unchanged (all criteria count).

**Communication modes.** Challenges with radio work set `commsModes`
(`simAtc`, `network`, `selfVoiced`). The brief includes a script for self-voiced mode.
Criteria are written so every mode can pass ("Read back every hold-short instruction").

**Logbook fields.** Every flight challenge declares what it contributes to the logbook:
`logbook: { night, crossCountry, instrument: 'simulated'|'none', approaches, holds,
landings }`. The debrief pre-fills these and lets the learner correct the flight time and
landing counts before submitting (Section 18.4).

**Anywhere mode (P1).** Manoeuvre challenges with `anywhere: true` (e.g. C17.1, C10.2) can
be flown at any airport; the brief replaces airport-specific setup with generic
instructions.

### 16.2 Rubric and scoring changes

The v1 algorithm (v1 Section 15.2) is unchanged. v2 adds two criterion kinds:

- **Auto** — the result is computed, not chosen. The learner enters planning answers
  (e.g. CG, takeoff distance) or completes a drill widget; the server compares with the
  authored answer and tolerance and assigns Gold/Silver/Bronze/Not met (tiered) or
  Met/Not met (binary). The client shows the same result live using `shared/`.
- **Decision** — a binary criterion about a judgement call, always with a clear, timed
  trigger ("Turned back or diverted before the cloud base was below 1,000 ft AGL"). The
  debrief shows the decision criterion first. When the decision is recorded as a planning
  answer (e.g. go/no-go in C11.1), it is graded automatically (`autoFrom`, Appendix C).

Planning answers for auto criteria are defined per variant in the challenge YAML:
`{ fieldId, answer, tolerance: { gold, silver, bronze }, units }` (Appendix C).

**Stage checks** (`type: stage-check`) list `gateChallenges`. If any are not yet passed,
the brief shows the stronger warning from v1 Section 13.3. Course completion needs the
stage check at Silver or better (Section 14.3).

### 16.3 How to read the specs below

As v1 Section 15.3. Criteria tables use: **Kind** (Tiered, Binary, Auto, Decision),
**Req**uired and **W**eight. Tolerances follow v1 Section 12.9 (VFR) or Section 13.7
(IFR) unless stated. P1 challenges have shorter specs; flesh them out when they are
scheduled. Every airport, runway, procedure and number is **⚠ verify**.

### Module 9 challenges

#### C9.1 — Hot day at Livermore (P0)

- **Type** planning · **Tags** performance · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 30 min ·
  **Lessons** L9.1, L9.2
- **Goal:** Work out density altitude and takeoff distance on a hot afternoon, then fly a
  departure that matches your numbers.
- **Setup:** C172 G1000 · KLVK · `RUNWAY` 25R · `WX_HOT` · `TIME_HOT_AFTERNOON` ·
  `LOAD_FULL`. Variants: 38 °C/29.82, 42 °C/29.78, 30 °C/29.95.
- **Procedure**
  1. Planning tab: enter pressure altitude, density altitude, ground roll and distance
     over 50 ft (W22 open alongside).
  2. In the sim: note the runway remaining marker when you lift off (or use replay).
  3. Climb at Vy to 3,000 ft; note the average climb rate.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                        | Kind   | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Density altitude calculated                                      | Auto   | ±200 ft | ±400 ft | ±600 ft | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Takeoff distance over 50 ft from W22                             | Auto   | ±5%     | ±10%    | ±15%    | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Lift-off point within your predicted ground roll +10%            | Binary |         |         |         | No  | 2   |
| 4   | Climb at Vy                                                      | Tiered | ±5 kt   | ±10 kt  | ±15 kt  | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | Leaned for best power per the checklist (if required at this DA) | Binary |         |         |         | No  | 1   |

- **Common mistakes:** forgetting the altimeter correction; reading the wrong temperature
  column; climbing too steeply and losing speed.
- **Debrief questions:** How much longer was the takeoff than on a standard day? Would you
  have taken off from a 2,500 ft runway today?

#### C9.2 — Plan the load (P0)

- **Type** planning · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 30 min · **Lessons** L9.3
- **Goal:** Compute weight and balance for a four-person trip, fix an out-of-limits
  load, then fly the loaded airplane and feel the difference.
- **Setup:** Variants give different passengers, bags and fuel (one variant is out of
  limits aft; one is over maximum weight). Flight: KLVK · `RUNWAY` 25R · `WX_CALM` ·
  load as planned.
- **Procedure**
  1. Planning tab (W23): enter each station weight; compute total weight, moment, CG.
  2. Decide: within limits? If not, choose a fix from the options (move bags, reduce
     fuel, leave a bag behind).
  3. Set the payload in the sim to your final plan; fly a pattern with a full-stop
     landing and note the pitch forces and trim.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                               | Kind     | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | --------------------------------------- | -------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Total weight correct                    | Auto     | ±5 lb   | ±15 lb  | ±30 lb  | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | CG correct                              | Auto     | ±0.2 in | ±0.5 in | ±1.0 in | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Correct go/fix decision for the variant | Decision |         |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Sim payload matches the plan            | Binary   |         |         |         | No  | 1   |
| 5   | Pattern flown to v1 C4.2 standards      | Tiered   | ±100 ft | ±150 ft | ±200 ft | No  | 1   |

- **Debrief questions:** How did the airplane feel compared with solo? Where does the CG
  move as fuel burns?

#### C9.3 — Lean for cruise and fuel check (P0)

- **Type** procedure · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L9.4
- **Goal:** Lean correctly in the climb and cruise and keep a fuel log on a
  KLVK → KMRY leg (landing optional).
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RUNWAY` 25R · `WX_LIGHT_W` · `LOAD_SOLO` · fuel 50%.
- **Procedure:** plan fuel (planning tab); climb to 6,500 ft leaning per the checklist;
  lean for cruise; record fuel on board at three checkpoints; compute remaining endurance
  at each.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                    | Kind   | Gold     | Silver  | Bronze   | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------- | ------ | -------- | ------- | -------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Planned fuel burn and reserve                | Auto   | ±1 gal   | ±2 gal  | ±3 gal   | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Cruise fuel flow within the checklist target | Tiered | ±0.5 gph | ±1 gph  | ±1.5 gph | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Fuel log at three checkpoints                | Binary |          |         |          | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Altitude held in cruise                      | Tiered | ±100 ft  | ±150 ft | ±200 ft  | No  | 1   |

### Module 10 challenges

#### C10.1 — Crosswind mastery pack (P0)

- **Type** landing · **Tags** crosswind · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 30 min per
  variant · **Lessons** L10.1 (and L4.5)
- **Goal:** Land safely in four increasingly hard crosswinds. Pass each variant to earn
  the pack.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RUNWAY` 25R (or 25L) · `WX_GUST_XW` variants: 8 kt, 12 kt, 12G18,
  15G22 · `LOAD_SOLO` · auto-rudder **off** (P1 variant for gamepad users with auto-rudder
  on is scored separately).
- **Procedure:** fly the pattern; compute the gust additive; crab on final, transition to
  wing-low; touch down on the upwind main first; aileron into the wind on the rollout; go
  around if not stabilised or beyond your limit.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                             | Kind     | Gold                       | Silver       | Bronze         | Req | W   |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------- | -------- | -------------------------- | ------------ | -------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Final speed including gust additive                   | Tiered   | −0/+5 kt                   | ±7 kt        | ±10 kt         | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | No side load at touchdown (longitudinal axis aligned) | Tiered   | Aligned, upwind main first | Slight drift | Drifting, safe | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Centreline at touchdown                               | Tiered   | ½ wingspan                 | 1 wingspan   | On the runway  | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Aileron into the wind through the rollout             | Binary   |                            |              |                | No  | 1   |
| 5   | Went around if not stabilised by 300 ft AGL           | Decision |                            |              |                | Yes | 2   |

- **Pack rule:** the pack counts as passed when every variant is passed (tracked by
  `variantId`). Progress for the challenge itself uses the best attempt (as v1).

#### C10.2 — Forward slip to a landing (P0)

- **Type** landing · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 20 min · **Lessons** L10.2 ·
  **Anywhere** P1
- **Goal:** Arrive high on final with flaps up, use a forward slip to reach a normal
  glide path, recover and land on your aiming point.
- **Setup:** KTCY · `AIR_START` 2 nm final runway 26 (⚠ verify), 1,800 ft MSL, 75 KIAS,
  flaps 0 · `WX_CALM`. Fallback: fly a high pattern from `RUNWAY`.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                   | Kind   | Gold              | Silver                | Bronze                 | Req | W   |
| --- | ------------------------------------------- | ------ | ----------------- | --------------------- | ---------------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Airspeed controlled in the slip             | Tiered | ±5 kt             | ±10 kt                | ±15 kt                 | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Ground track stayed aligned with the runway | Tiered | Centreline        | Within 1 runway width | Returned to centreline | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Slip removed before touchdown               | Binary |                   |                       |                        | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Touchdown point                             | Tiered | ≤ 400 ft past aim | First third           | On the runway          | Yes | 2   |

#### C10.3 — Soft-field takeoff and landing (P0)

- **Type** landing · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L10.3
- **Goal:** Taxi, take off and land using soft-field technique.
- **Setup:** KTCY · `RAMP_RUNNING` · `WX_CALM` · `LOAD_SOLO`. Variant B (P1): a grass
  strip found during Week 2 (⚠ record ICAO).
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                            | Kind   | Gold                              | Silver                | Bronze     | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | --------------------------------- | --------------------- | ---------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Taxied onto the runway without stopping, elevator back               | Binary |                                   |                       |            | No  | 1   |
| 2   | Lift-off at the lowest practical speed; accelerated in ground effect | Tiered | Held within 10 ft AGL to Vx       | Brief climb before Vx | Safe climb | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Touchdown at minimum speed, nose held off                            | Tiered | Nose wheel held off to slow speed | Nose down early       | Safe, flat | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Kept power as needed during the landing roll                         | Binary |                                   |                       |            | No  | 1   |

#### C10.4 — Short field over an obstacle (P0)

- **Type** landing · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 30 min · **Lessons** L10.4 (and L4.6)
- **Goal:** Plan with W22, take off over a (simulated) 50-ft obstacle, then land short at
  KPAO.
- **Setup:** KPAO · `RUNWAY` 31 (⚠ verify) · `WX_LIGHT_W` · `LOAD_FULL`. Planning tab:
  takeoff and landing distances.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                       | Kind   | Gold     | Silver   | Bronze   | Req | W   |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------ | -------- | -------- | -------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Planned distances correct (W22)                 | Auto   | ±5%      | ±10%     | ±15%     | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Vx until clear of the obstacle, then Vy         | Tiered | ±3 kt    | ±5 kt    | ±10 kt   | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Short-field final speed                         | Tiered | −0/+5 kt | ±5 kt    | ±10 kt   | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Touchdown within 200 ft beyond the aiming point | Tiered | 0–100 ft | 0–200 ft | 0–400 ft | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | Maximum braking without skidding                | Binary |          |          |          | No  | 1   |

#### C10.5 — Hot-spot taxi at Oakland (P1)

- **Type** procedure · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L10.5 · **Setup:** KOAK general
  aviation ramp (⚠ verify location) · `RAMP_RUNNING` · sim ATC or self-voiced.
- **Goal:** brief the taxi route and hot spots from the airport diagram, taxi to the
  assigned runway, read back every hold-short instruction. **Criteria:** taxi brief
  completed (binary, req), correct route (binary, req), every hold-short read back and
  complied with (binary, req, W3), taxi speed appropriate (binary).

#### C10.6 — Power-off 180 (P1)

- **Type** landing · **Est.** 20 min · **Lessons** L10.6 · **Setup:** KTCY pattern ·
  `WX_CALM`. **Goal:** power to idle abeam the aiming point, land within 200 ft beyond it
  without adding power. **Criteria:** touchdown distance (tiered 0–100/200/400 ft, req,
  W3), no power added (binary, req), speed control (tiered).

### Module 11 challenges

#### C11.1 — Go / no-go board (P0)

- **Type** planning · **Tags** weather, decision · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.**
  20 min per variant · **Lessons** L11.2, L11.6
- **Goal:** Read a full briefing for a planned flight and make the right decision.
- **Setup:** no sim. Each variant is a curated **weather scenario** (Section 27.8) for a
  KLVK → KMRY flight: METARs, TAFs, GFA summary, winds aloft, AIRMETs, PIREPs. Variants
  include a clear "go", a "no-go" (marine layer at the destination below minimums), a
  "delay" (fog burning off), and a tricky "go with a different route".
- **Procedure:** read the briefing (W17 decodes on click); choose Go / No-go / Delay /
  Go with changes; tick the limiting factors; write your personal minimums for this
  flight.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                             | Kind   | Gold               | Silver         | Bronze | Req | W   |
| --- | ------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------------ | -------------- | ------ | --- | --- |
| 1   | Correct decision for the scenario     | Auto   | (binary)           |                |        | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Limiting factors identified           | Auto   | All, no wrong ones | All, one wrong | Most   | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Personal minimums written and applied | Binary |                    |                |        | No  | 1   |

- The result screen shows the authored explanation for each scenario.

#### C11.2 — Marine layer at Half Moon Bay (P0)

- **Type** navigation · **Tags** weather, decision · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.**
  30 min · **Lessons** L11.3, L11.6
- **Goal:** Fly KPAO → KHAF over the hills. The marine layer is lower than forecast. Make
  the safe decision.
- **Setup:** KPAO · `RUNWAY` 31 · `WX_MARINE_LAYER` (variants: layer at 1,200 ft — landable
  with care; layer at 600 ft — must divert) · `LOAD_SOLO`.
- **Procedure:** brief a turn-back point and an alternate (KSQL or back to KPAO) before
  departure; fly the route; decide at or before the turn-back point.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                | Kind     | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Turn-back point and alternate briefed before departure   | Binary   |         |         |         | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Maintained VFR cloud clearances throughout               | Binary   |         |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Made the right decision at or before the turn-back point | Decision |         |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Altitude above terrain and below the cloud per the plan  | Tiered   | ±100 ft | ±150 ft | ±200 ft | No  | 1   |

#### C11.3 — Inadvertent IMC: the 180 (P0)

- **Type** manoeuvre · **Tags** weather, instrument · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.**
  15 min · **Lessons** L11.6
- **Goal:** Fly into a cloud bank by accident (deliberately, in the sim) and get out with a
  coordinated 180° turn on instruments.
- **Setup:** `AIR_START` 3,500 ft east of KLVK heading 090 · `WX_CLOUD_BANK` · event: a
  `prompt` at 60–180 s "You're in cloud — execute the 180".
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                    | Kind   | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Wings levelled and attitude stabilised first | Binary |         |         |         | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Bank during the turn                         | Tiered | 15–20°  | ≤ 25°   | ≤ 30°   | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Altitude held                                | Tiered | ±100 ft | ±200 ft | ±300 ft | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Rolled out on the reciprocal heading         | Tiered | ±10°    | ±15°    | ±20°    | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | Called for help (ATC or 121.5) — spoken      | Binary |         |         |         | No  | 1   |

#### C11.4 — Live-weather cross-country (P1)

- **Type** navigation · **Tags** weather, live · **Est.** 60 min · **Lessons** L11.5 ·
  **Setup:** live weather on; a KLVK → KMRY → KLVK route (or any v1 route). **Goal:** do a
  real briefing (links in the brief), record the decision and actual conditions, fly it
  if it is a go. **Criteria:** briefing completed (binary, req), decision recorded with
  reasons (decision, req), flown within VFR minimums (binary, req), nav log kept (binary).

### Module 12 challenges

#### C12.1 — Night currency circuits (P0)

- **Type** night · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L12.3
- **Goal:** Three night full-stop landings at Livermore — the same as a real pilot's
  night currency.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RAMP_RUNNING` · `WX_NIGHT_CLEAR` · `TIME_NIGHT` (variant:
  `TIME_NIGHT_DARK`) · sim ATC or self-voiced.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                            | Kind   | Gold        | Silver  | Bronze         | Req | W   |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ----------- | ------- | -------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Correct aircraft lighting for each phase             | Binary |             |         |                | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Pattern altitude                                     | Tiered | ±100 ft     | ±150 ft | ±200 ft        | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Stabilised on each final, on the PAPI                | Tiered | 3/3 on path | 2/3     | 1/3, rest safe | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Three full-stop landings, each in the first third    | Tiered | 3/3         | 2/3     | 3 safe         | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | One landing without the landing light (variant step) | Binary |             |         |                | No  | 1   |

- **Logbook:** night true, landings 3 (night, full stop).

#### C12.2 — Pilot-controlled lighting at Tracy (P0)

- **Type** night · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L12.2
- **Goal:** Arrive at a dark, non-towered airport, turn on the runway lights with the
  mic, make CTAF calls and land.
- **Setup:** `AIR_START` 10 nm west of KTCY 3,000 ft (fallback: depart KLVK) ·
  `TIME_NIGHT_DARK` · `WX_NIGHT_CLEAR` · AI traffic on.
- **Criteria:** PCL activated (7 clicks) before entering the pattern (binary, req — "Sim vs
  reality" note if not modelled); CTAF calls at the standard positions (tiered 5/4/3 of 5,
  req); pattern entry per AIM (binary, req); PAPI/VASI used (binary); landing
  (tiered as v1 C4.3, req).

#### C12.3 — Night cross-country to Watsonville (P0)

- **Type** navigation · **Tags** night · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 70 min ·
  **Lessons** L12.4
- **Goal:** Plan and fly KLVK → KWVI → KLVK at night with lit checkpoints and a 45-minute
  reserve.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RAMP_RUNNING` · `TIME_NIGHT` · `WX_NIGHT_CLEAR` (variant: scattered
  clouds over the Santa Cruz Mountains at 4,500 ft) · `LOAD_SOLO` · legs: Outbound,
  Watsonville, Return.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                             | Kind   | Gold                      | Silver              | Bronze         | Req | W   |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------------------- | ------------------- | -------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Nav log with lit checkpoints and 45-min reserve       | Auto   | Fuel ±1 gal, times ±2 min | ±2 gal, ±4 min      | ±3 gal, ±6 min | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Altitude above the planned terrain clearance altitude | Tiered | ±100 ft                   | ±150 ft             | ±200 ft        | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Checkpoints within the planned time                   | Tiered | ±2 min                    | ±4 min              | ±6 min         | No  | 1   |
| 4   | Black-hole approach flown on the PAPI/VASI            | Binary |                           |                     |                | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | Landings (KWVI and KLVK)                              | Tiered | Both first third          | Both safe, one long | Both safe      | Yes | 2   |

#### C12.4 — Night alternator failure (P1)

- **Type** emergency · **Tags** night, failure · **Est.** 30 min · **Lessons** L12.4,
  L14.1 · **Setup:** C12.3 route outbound leg · `failure` event (alternator) at 5–15 min.
  **Criteria:** failure recognised within 2 min (binary, req), load shed per flow
  (binary, req), decision to land at the nearest suitable lit airport (decision, req),
  landing (tiered).

### Module 13 challenges

#### C13.1 — Truckee on a hot afternoon (P0)

- **Type** planning · **Tags** mountain, performance · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.**
  30 min · **Lessons** L13.3
- **Goal:** Compute density altitude and takeoff performance at Truckee, lean for takeoff,
  depart and out-climb the terrain.
- **Setup:** KTRK · `RUNWAY` (⚠ verify runway) · `WX_HOT_SIERRA` · `TIME_HOT_AFTERNOON` ·
  `LOAD_FULL` (variant `LOAD_SOLO`).
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                | Kind     | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Density altitude                                         | Auto     | ±200 ft | ±400 ft | ±600 ft | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Takeoff distance and abort point planned                 | Auto     | ±5%     | ±10%    | ±15%    | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Leaned for takeoff per the method taught                 | Binary   |         |         |         | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Abort decision: continued only if abort criteria met     | Decision |         |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | Climb at Vy, turning over the valley, not toward terrain | Tiered   | ±5 kt   | ±10 kt  | ±15 kt  | Yes | 2   |

#### C13.2 — Donner Pass crossing (P0)

- **Type** navigation · **Tags** mountain · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 60 min ·
  **Lessons** L13.1, L13.2
- **Goal:** Fly KAUN → Donner Summit → KTRK along I-80, crossing the ridge safely with an
  escape route at all times.
- **Setup:** KAUN · `RUNWAY` · `TIME_DAWN` · `WX_MOUNTAIN_CALM` (variant: winds 25 kt at
  ridge level — decision variant) · `LOAD_SOLO` · legs: Climb, Pass, Arrival.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                      | Kind     | Gold                    | Silver          | Bronze                   | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------ | --- | --- |
| 1   | Planned crossing altitude and escape routes on the kneeboard   | Binary   |                         |                 |                          | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Crossed the ridge at or above the planned altitude             | Tiered   | ≥ plan                  | ≥ plan − 200 ft | ≥ 1,000 ft above terrain | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Approached the ridge at about 45°                              | Binary   |                         |                 |                          | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Right decision in the high-wind variant (turn back or reroute) | Decision |                         |                 |                          | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | Arrival and landing at KTRK                                    | Tiered   | Stabilised, first third | Stabilised      | Safe                     | No  | 1   |

#### C13.3 — Arrival at South Lake Tahoe (P0)

- **Type** landing · **Tags** mountain · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 30 min ·
  **Lessons** L13.3
- **Goal:** Fly from KTRK along the lake to KTVL and land with a high groundspeed on
  final.
- **Setup:** KTRK · `RUNWAY` · `WX_MOUNTAIN_CALM` · `TIME_DAY`.
- **Criteria:** lean for climb and descent (binary); pattern at KTVL pattern altitude
  (tiered, req); final at the normal **indicated** speed (tiered, req, W3); go-around
  briefed for the density altitude (binary, req); touchdown in the first third (tiered,
  req).

#### C13.4 — Mountain wave (P1)

- **Type** navigation · **Tags** mountain, decision · **Est.** 30 min · **Lessons**
  L13.1 · **Setup:** eastbound over the Carson Range toward KMEV · `WX_MOUNTAIN_WAVE`.
  **Goal:** recognise lee-side sink and turbulence, slow to Va, turn toward lower terrain
  and away from the lee, and choose not to continue. **Criteria:** recognised sink early
  (decision, req), slowed to Va (binary, req), turned away safely (binary, req), terrain
  clearance kept (tiered).

### Module 14 challenges

Failures use `failure` events (D2-09). Every Module 14 challenge starts with the learner
**not** knowing when the failure will occur.

#### C14.1 — Alternator failure (P0)

- **Type** emergency · **Tags** failure · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 35 min ·
  **Lessons** L14.1
- **Setup:** KLVK → KSNS cross-country (v1 C8.2 style route) · `WX_LIGHT_W` ·
  `failure` event "Alternator failure — switch ALT off" at 5–20 min.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                  | Kind     | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | ------------------------------------------ | -------- | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Recognised the failure (EIS, annunciation) | Tiered   | < 1 min | < 3 min | < 5 min | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Flew the checklist flow in order           | Binary   |         |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Shed non-essential load                    | Binary   |         |         |         | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Diverted to the nearest suitable airport   | Decision |         |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | Aircraft control maintained throughout     | Tiered   | ±100 ft | ±150 ft | ±200 ft | Yes | 2   |

#### C14.2 — PFD failure (P0)

- **Type** emergency · **Tags** failure · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 25 min ·
  **Lessons** L14.2
- **Setup:** `AIR_START` 4,500 ft over the Livermore valley · `WX_LIGHT_W` · `failure`
  event "PFD failed — press DISPLAY BACKUP" at 1–5 min.
- **Criteria:** reversionary mode selected (binary, req, W3); heading and altitude held
  during the transition (tiered, req, W3); returned to KLVK and landed (tiered, req);
  briefed the remaining displays aloud (binary).

#### C14.3 — Blocked pitot tube (P0)

- **Type** emergency · **Tags** failure · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 25 min ·
  **Lessons** L14.2
- **Setup:** KLVK pattern · `WX_CALM`. Realism mode: sim pitot failure (⚠ verify).
  Default: `failure` card "Airspeed unreliable — cover the airspeed tape", flown on
  pitch, power and the known power table.
- **Criteria:** recognised unreliable airspeed (binary, req); set known pitch/power for
  each phase (tiered by resulting altitude/descent consistency, req, W3); stabilised
  approach and landing using pitch and power (tiered, req, W3); used GPS groundspeed as a
  cross-check (binary).

#### C14.4 — Rough running engine (P0)

- **Type** emergency · **Tags** failure · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 25 min ·
  **Lessons** L14.3
- **Setup:** `AIR_START` 3,500 ft between KLVK and KTCY · `failure` event "Engine rough —
  pull mixture back to the rough point and leave it" (variant: "reduce throttle to 1,800
  RPM and leave it").
- **Criteria:** best glide/pitch for the available power (tiered, req); troubleshooting
  flow in order (binary, req, W3); decision to land at the nearest suitable airport
  (decision, req, W3); landing (tiered, req).

#### C14.5 — Engine fire and emergency descent (P0)

- **Type** emergency · **Tags** failure · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 25 min ·
  **Lessons** L14.4
- **Setup:** `AIR_START` 6,500 ft over the Central Valley · `failure` event "ENGINE FIRE"
  at 2–8 min.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                     | Kind   | Gold        | Silver            | Bronze     | Req | W   |
| --- | --------------------------------------------- | ------ | ----------- | ----------------- | ---------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Memory items flown in order                   | Binary |             |                   |            | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Emergency descent within limits (speed, bank) | Tiered | ±5 kt       | ±10 kt            | Within Vne | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Field chosen and forced landing planned       | Binary |             |                   |            | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Touchdown in the chosen field, under control  | Tiered | First third | Anywhere in field | Survivable | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | Mayday call and 7700 (spoken/set)             | Binary |             |                   |            | No  | 1   |

#### C14.6 — Surprise failure (P1)

- **Type** emergency · **Est.** 40 min · **Setup:** any Module 14 route · a `failure` event
  chosen randomly from the Module 14 set. **Goal:** handle whatever happens. **Criteria:**
  the union of the matching challenge's required criteria (authored per variant).

### Module 15 challenges

#### C15.1 — Class Bravo transition (P0)

- **Type** communication · **Tags** airspace · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 45 min ·
  **Lessons** L15.1 · **commsModes** simAtc, network, selfVoiced
- **Goal:** Get a Class B clearance and fly a transition over the bay (⚠ design the route
  after checking the current TAC and any published VFR transition), then return.
- **Setup:** KHWD · `RAMP_RUNNING` · `WX_LIGHT_W` · AI traffic on.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                           | Kind   | Gold    | Silver      | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------- | ----------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Did not enter Class B before hearing "cleared into" | Binary |         |             |         | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Correct request call (who, where, what, request)    | Binary |         |             |         | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Readbacks of altitude, heading and squawk           | Tiered | All     | All but one | Most    | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Assigned altitude held                              | Tiered | ±100 ft | ±150 ft     | ±200 ft | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | Assigned headings/route flown                       | Tiered | ±10°    | ±15°        | ±20°    | No  | 1   |

#### C15.2 — Flight following to Monterey (P0)

- **Type** communication · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 55 min · **Lessons** L15.2 ·
  **commsModes** all three
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RAMP_RUNNING` · `WX_LIGHT_W` · AI traffic on · events: two scripted
  traffic calls (`atc`) in self-voiced mode.
- **Criteria:** request made correctly (binary, req); squawk and altitude readbacks
  (tiered, req); traffic calls answered correctly (binary, req); handoffs completed
  (binary); arrival at KMRY per tower/ATIS instructions (binary, req); landing (tiered).

#### C15.3 — Readback drill (P0)

- **Type** drill · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 10 min · **Lessons** L15.4
- **Goal:** Read back 10 instructions correctly in W32 (typed; speech synthesis reads
  them out).
- **Setup:** no sim. Variants: VFR ground, VFR airborne, Class B/C.
- **Criteria:** readbacks with every required element (auto, tiered 10/10, 9, 8, req,
  W3); no extra or wrong numbers (auto, binary, req).

#### C15.4 — Radio failure at a towered airport (P1)

- **Type** communication · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L15.3 · **Setup:** KLVK arrival
  from the east; `failure` event "COM failure — squawk 7600". Light gun signals are shown
  by the Fly tab as `prompt` events (sim towers may not model them; ⚠ verify).
  **Criteria:** 7600 set (binary, req), correct pattern entry and watching the tower
  (binary, req), correct response to each light signal (auto from a quick-pick, req),
  landing (tiered).

#### C15.5 — Your first VATSIM flight (P1)

- **Type** communication · **Tags** network · **Est.** 45 min · **Lessons** L15.5 ·
  **Setup:** any Bay Area towered airport with a controller online, or an uncontrolled
  airport using UNICOM. **Goal:** file a VFR flight plan, connect, get taxi and takeoff
  clearance (or self-announce), fly a pattern or short flight, disconnect politely.
  **Criteria:** Code of Conduct followed (binary, req); flight plan filed (binary);
  correct calls and readbacks (tiered, req); pattern flown to standard (tiered).

### Module 16 challenges

#### C16.1 — Advanced VFR stage check (P0)

- **Type** stage-check · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 75 min · **Lessons** L16.2 ·
  **gateChallenges** all P0 challenges in Modules 9–15
- **Goal:** Show the examiner (you) the Course 2 skills in one flight.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RAMP_RUNNING` · variant-selected weather (`WX_GUST_XW` 12 kt or
  12G18) · `LOAD_FULL` · events: one random `failure` from {alternator, rough engine,
  PFD} at 10–30 min; `prompt` "Show me a forward slip to landing" on the last circuit ·
  legs: Planning, Departure, Airwork, Emergency, Landings.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                   | Kind     | Gold              | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Performance and W&B planned correctly                       | Auto     | As C9.1/C9.2 Gold | Silver  | Bronze  | Yes | 2   |
| 2   | Short-field takeoff                                         | Tiered   | ±3 kt, Vx→Vy      | ±5 kt   | ±10 kt  | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Steep turns (v1 C5.4 standard)                              | Tiered   | ±100 ft, ±10 kt   | ±150 ft | ±200 ft | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Failure handled per its challenge's flow                    | Binary   |                   |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | Right decision after the failure                            | Decision |                   |         |         | Yes | 3   |
| 6   | Crosswind landing (C10.1 criteria 2–3)                      | Tiered   | Aligned, ½ span   | 1 span  | Safe    | Yes | 2   |
| 7   | Forward slip to landing                                     | Tiered   | As C10.2          |         |         | Yes | 2   |
| 8   | Soft- or short-field landing (examiner's choice by variant) | Tiered   | As C10.3/C10.4    |         |         | No  | 1   |

#### C16.2 — Bay to the Sierra (P0)

- **Type** capstone · **Tags** mountain, navigation · **Difficulty** ●●●●● · **Est.**
  120 min (can pause between legs) · **Lessons** L16.1, L16.2
- **Goal:** Plan and fly KLVK → KAUN → Donner Pass → KTRK, with flight following through
  the Sacramento area, and return via KTVL if the weather and daylight allow.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `RAMP_RUNNING` · `TIME_DAWN` · `WX_MOUNTAIN_CALM` (variant with
  afternoon build-ups — decision variant) · `LOAD_FULL` · legs: Valley, Foothills, Pass,
  Truckee, Return (optional) · event: a `prompt` diversion ("Truckee closed — divert")
  with a 30% chance.
- **Planning fields (auto):** DA at KTRK, takeoff distance at KTRK, W&B, fuel with reserve,
  crossing altitude.
- **Criteria:** planning (auto, req, W2); flight following through the valley (binary,
  req); pass crossing per C13.2 criteria 2–3 (tiered, req, W3); high-DA landing at KTRK
  (tiered, req); diversion or weather decision if the event fires (decision, req);
  return-leg go/no-go decision recorded (decision); overall nav log accuracy (tiered).

### Module 17 challenges

IFR tolerances (Section 13.7) apply from here on. All Course 3 challenges set
`logbook.instrument: 'simulated'` and record approaches and holds.

#### C17.1 — Basic instrument manoeuvres (P0)

- **Type** instrument · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L17.1,
  L17.2 · **Anywhere** P1
- **Goal:** Fly the basic manoeuvres by instruments only.
- **Setup:** `AIR_START_IMC` 4,000 ft in `WX_IMC_HIGH` near KLVK (above the base, below
  the tops), heading 090, 100 KIAS.
- **Procedure:** 2 min straight and level; standard-rate turn to 270; constant-airspeed
  climb (80 KIAS) to 5,000 ft; constant-rate descent (500 fpm, 90 KIAS) to 4,000 ft;
  timed 90° turn using the clock only.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                       | Kind   | Gold    | Silver  | Bronze  | Req | W   |
| --- | ------------------------------- | ------ | ------- | ------- | ------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Altitude in level segments      | Tiered | ±100 ft | ±150 ft | ±200 ft | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Heading in straight segments    | Tiered | ±10°    | ±15°    | ±20°    | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Airspeed in climbs and descents | Tiered | ±10 kt  | ±10 kt  | ±15 kt  | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Level-offs                      | Tiered | ±50 ft  | ±100 ft | ±150 ft | No  | 1   |
| 5   | Timed turn rollout              | Tiered | ±10°    | ±15°    | ±20°    | Yes | 2   |

#### C17.2 — Pattern A (P0)

- **Type** instrument · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L17.2
- **Goal:** Fly Pattern A (timed legs, standard-rate turns, one climb/descent segment;
  ⚠ verify the version in the lesson) with a stopwatch.
- **Setup:** as C17.1 · stopwatch on the Fly tab shows each leg's target time.
- **Criteria:** leg timing (tiered ±5/10/15 s, req, W2); heading at each rollout (tiered,
  req, W2); altitude (tiered, req, W3); airspeed changes completed within the leg
  (binary, W1).

#### C17.3 — Unusual attitudes (P0)

- **Type** instrument · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 20 min · **Lessons** L17.3
- **Goal:** Recover from four unusual attitudes you did not see develop.
- **Setup:** `AIR_START` 5,500 ft, `WX_IMC_HIGH` · four `lookAway` events: "Look away from
  the screen. Roll left gently and pull back for 8 seconds, then release. Look back at
  the beep." (variants mix nose-high/nose-low and left/right).
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                                              | Kind   | Gold     | Silver   | Bronze           | Req | W   |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | -------- | -------- | ---------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Correct recovery order for each attitude                               | Tiered | 4/4      | 3/4      | 2/4, others safe | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | No overstress (speed within limits, gentle pull-out)                   | Binary |          |          |                  | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Altitude lost in nose-low recoveries                                   | Tiered | < 300 ft | < 500 ft | < 800 ft         | No  | 1   |
| 4   | Returned to straight and level, then to the start heading and altitude | Binary |          |          |                  | No  | 1   |

#### C17.4 — Partial panel (P0)

- **Type** instrument · **Tags** failure · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 30 min ·
  **Lessons** L17.4
- **Setup:** `AIR_START_IMC` · `failure` event "AHRS failure — cover the PFD attitude and
  heading (or use the sim failure)" at 1–3 min.
- **Procedure:** straight and level on standby instruments; timed turn to a heading;
  compass turn to north and to south; a constant-rate descent of 500 ft.
- **Criteria:** altitude (tiered, req, W3); timed-turn rollout (tiered, req, W2); compass
  turns with correct lead/lag (tiered ±10°/±20°/±30°, req, W2); no loss of control
  (binary, req, W3).

#### C17.5 — Pattern B (P1)

- **Type** instrument · **Est.** 30 min · **Lessons** L17.2 · As C17.2 with the Pattern B
  profile (includes a simulated approach and missed-approach segment).

### Module 18 challenges

#### C18.1 — Copy a clearance (P0)

- **Type** drill · **Difficulty** ●●○○○ · **Est.** 10 min · **Lessons** L18.3
- **Goal:** Copy five IFR clearances in CRAFT shorthand and read them back.
- **Setup:** no sim. W32 in clearance mode (speech synthesis reads each clearance once;
  "say again" allowed once, recorded). Variants: short local, airway routing, "cleared as
  filed", amended route, void time.
- **Criteria:** every CRAFT element correct (auto, tiered 5/5, 4, 3, req, W3); readback
  complete (auto, binary, req, W2); "say again" used at most once (binary, W1).

#### C18.2 — Program the box (P0)

- **Type** procedure · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 25 min · **Lessons** L18.4
- **Goal:** Set up the G1000 for an IFR departure and arrival before engine start.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `IFR_RAMP` · the brief lists the clearance, route and expected
  approach.
- **Procedure:** a checklist of 14 items ticked on the page (like v1 C1.1): flight plan
  entered and checked against the clearance, departure procedure (if any), approach
  loaded, NAV frequencies tuned and identified, CDI source, altitude preselect, heading
  bug, transponder code, timer, standby frequencies, altimeter, autopilot modes briefed,
  missed approach reviewed, fuel check.
- **Criteria:** items completed (tiered 14/14, 12–13, 10–11, req, W3); time taken (tiered
  ≤ 10/15/20 min, W1); plan matches clearance with no discontinuities (binary, req, W3).

#### C18.3 — IFR departure and enroute (P0)

- **Type** instrument · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 45 min · **Lessons** L18.3, L18.5
- **Goal:** Depart KLVK in cloud following the departure procedure, join the route and fly
  it to the first fix of your arrival.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `IFR_RAMP` · `WX_IMC_HIGH` · commsModes all three · legs: Departure,
  Enroute.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                    | Kind   | Gold      | Silver       | Bronze           | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------------------- | ------ | --------- | ------------ | ---------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Departure procedure / ODP flown as published | Binary |           |              |                  | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Course tracking (CDI)                        | Tiered | ≤ ¾ scale | ≤ full scale | Brief full-scale | Yes | 3   |
| 3   | Assigned altitudes                           | Tiered | ±100 ft   | ±150 ft      | ±200 ft          | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Readbacks of every clearance element         | Tiered | All       | All but one  | Most             | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | 5 Ts at each fix                             | Binary |           |              |                  | No  | 1   |

### Module 19 challenges

#### C19.1 — Hold at a VOR (P0)

- **Type** holding · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L19.1
- **Goal:** Receive a holding clearance, choose the entry, and fly two turns in the hold
  by hand with wind correction.
- **Setup:** `AIR_START_IMC` 10 nm north-east of ECA (⚠ verify terrain and airspace) ·
  `WX_IMC_HIGH` with wind 270° at 20 kt · `atc` event "Hold south of ECA on the 180
  radial, right turns, maintain 4,000, expect further clearance in 15 minutes."
  Variants change the radial so that each entry type is required.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                        | Kind   | Gold        | Silver       | Bronze           | Req | W   |
| --- | -------------------------------- | ------ | ----------- | ------------ | ---------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Correct entry for the variant    | Auto   | (binary)    |              |                  | Yes | 3   |
| 2   | Inbound leg timing               | Tiered | 1 min ±15 s | ±30 s        | ±45 s            | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Inbound course tracking          | Tiered | ≤ ¾ scale   | ≤ full scale | Brief full scale | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Altitude                         | Tiered | ±100 ft     | ±150 ft      | ±200 ft          | Yes | 2   |
| 5   | Wind correction applied outbound | Binary |             |              |                  | No  | 1   |

- The entry chosen is entered on the debrief as a quick-pick; the server checks it
  against the variant (auto).

#### C19.2 — GPS hold (P0)

- **Type** holding · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 30 min · **Lessons** L19.2
- **Setup:** `AIR_START_IMC` near the KLVK approach IAF (⚠ verify a published hold or use
  a user hold) · `WX_IMC_HIGH`.
- **Procedure:** load the hold in the G1000; fly the entry by hand; then one turn coupled
  to the autopilot; exit the hold when "cleared for the approach" (event).
- **Criteria:** hold loaded correctly (binary, req, W2); hand-flown entry and first turn
  (tiered as C19.1, req, W3); autopilot modes correct (binary, req, W2); exit and
  sequencing to the approach (binary, req, W2).

### Module 20 challenges

Every approach challenge's brief shows the procedure name, the d-TPP cycle it was
verified against, a link to the current plate (D2-10) and the approach briefing card
(kneeboard). Variants change the weather between "breaks out above minimums" and "below
minimums" (`WX_IMC_BELOW`), so the learner never knows whether they will land.

#### C20.1 — RNAV (GPS) approach (P0)

- **Type** approach · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L20.2
- **Setup:** `AIR_START_IMC` 15 nm from the IAF of the KLVK RNAV (GPS) approach (⚠ verify
  procedure and IAF) · variants `WX_IMC_LOW`, `WX_IMC_BELOW` · commsModes all three.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                               | Kind     | Gold      | Silver       | Bronze           | Req | W   |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | --------- | ------------ | ---------------- | --- | --- |
| 1   | Approach briefed before the IAF                         | Binary   |           |              |                  | Yes | 1   |
| 2   | Approach mode annunciation checked before the FAF       | Binary   |           |              |                  | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Lateral tracking                                        | Tiered   | ≤ ¾ scale | ≤ full scale | Brief full scale | Yes | 3   |
| 4   | Vertical tracking on the glidepath                      | Tiered   | ≤ ¾ scale | ≤ full scale | Brief full scale | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | At DA: landed with runway in sight, or went missed      | Decision |           |              |                  | Yes | 3   |
| 6   | Did not descend below DA without the runway environment | Binary   |           |              |                  | Yes | 3   |
| 7   | Airspeed on final                                       | Tiered   | ±10 kt    | ±10 kt       | ±15 kt           | No  | 1   |

#### C20.2 — Hand-flown ILS (P0)

- **Type** approach · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L20.3
- **Setup:** KLVK ILS or LOC RWY 25R (⚠ verify) · vectors-to-final start: `AIR_START_IMC`
  on a 30° intercept heading to the localizer at the intercept altitude · variants
  `WX_IMC_LOW`, `WX_IMC_BELOW` · autopilot **off**.
- **Criteria:** as C20.1, with "ILS identified (Morse)" replacing criterion 2, and
  localizer/glideslope in criteria 3–4.

#### C20.3 — Coupled ILS (P0)

- **Type** approach · **Difficulty** ●●●○○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L20.3,
  L18.4
