- **Setup:** KMRY ILS or LOC RWY 10R (⚠ verify) · full procedure from the IAF with the
  autopilot in NAV/APR · `WX_IMC_LOW`.
- **Criteria:** correct autopilot mode sequence (armed → captured) monitored and called
  out (binary, req, W3); intervened within 5 s of any wrong mode (binary, req, W2);
  disconnected at or before DA (binary, req, W2); DA decision (decision, req, W3);
  landing or missed approach flown by hand (tiered, req, W2).

#### C20.4 — VOR approach (P0)

- **Type** approach · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 35 min · **Lessons** L20.4
- **Setup:** a VOR or VOR/DME approach from Section 12.4 (⚠ choose after verification) ·
  `WX_IMC_NP` (variant `WX_IMC_BELOW`).
- **Criteria:** VOR tuned and identified, CDI source VOR (binary, req); course tracking
  (tiered, req, W3); step-downs / CDFA to MDA (tiered +100/−0 Gold, req, W3); missed
  approach point identified (binary, req, W2); MDA decision (decision, req, W3).

#### C20.5 — Missed approach to the hold (P0)

- **Type** approach · **Tags** holding · **Difficulty** ●●●●○ · **Est.** 40 min ·
  **Lessons** L20.5, L19.1
- **Setup:** as C20.2 with `WX_IMC_BELOW` only — the missed approach is certain; the brief
  says "expect to go missed".
- **Criteria:** missed approach initiated at DA (binary, req, W3); flow in order —
  power, pitch, positive rate, flaps, navigate, talk (binary, req, W3); published missed
  approach course and altitude (tiered, req, W2); correct hold entry and first turn
  (tiered as C19.1, req, W2); ATC told / self-voiced call (binary, W1).

#### C20.6 — Partial-panel approach (P1)

- **Type** approach · **Tags** failure · **Est.** 40 min · **Lessons** L20.5, L17.4 ·
  **Setup:** KSCK ILS 29R or another verified LOC/ILS · `failure` event "PFD failed" before
  the FAF. **Criteria:** reversion or standby flying (binary, req), localizer tracking
  (tiered, req), DA decision (decision, req), no loss of control (binary, req).

#### C20.7 — Circle to land (P1)

- **Type** approach · **Est.** 40 min · **Lessons** L20.6 · **Setup:** a verified approach
  with circling minima (Section 12.4) · `WX_IMC_CIRCLE`. **Criteria:** circling MDA held
  (tiered, req), runway kept in sight (binary, req), stabilised on final (binary, req),
  landing (tiered).

### Module 21 challenges

#### C21.1 — Instrument stage check (P0)

- **Type** stage-check · **Difficulty** ●●●●● · **Est.** 90 min · **Lessons** L21.2 ·
  **gateChallenges** all P0 challenges in Modules 17–20
- **Setup:** KLVK · `IFR_RAMP` · variant weather · commsModes all three · legs: Clearance,
  Departure, Hold, Approach 1 (hand-flown, partial panel via `failure` event), Missed
  approach, Approach 2 (coupled, to landing) · random `failure` (alternator) with 30%
  chance on Approach 2.
- **Criteria**

| #   | Criterion                                         | Kind     | Gold          | Silver       | Bronze | Req | W   |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------- | ------------ | ------ | --- | --- |
| 1   | Clearance copied and read back                    | Auto     | As C18.1 Gold | Silver       | Bronze | Yes | 1   |
| 2   | Departure and enroute tracking and altitudes      | Tiered   | As C18.3      |              |        | Yes | 2   |
| 3   | Hold: entry, timing, tracking                     | Tiered   | As C19.1      |              |        | Yes | 2   |
| 4   | Approach 1 on partial panel                       | Tiered   | ≤ ¾ scale     | ≤ full scale | Safe   | Yes | 3   |
| 5   | Missed approach flow and procedure                | Binary   |               |              |        | Yes | 3   |
| 6   | Approach 2 automation management                  | Binary   |               |              |        | Yes | 2   |
| 7   | Never below DA/MDA without the runway environment | Binary   |               |              |        | Yes | 3   |
| 8   | Failure handled and right decision made           | Decision |               |              |        | No  | 2   |

#### C21.2 — IFR cross-country capstone (P0)

- **Type** capstone · **Tags** ifr · **Difficulty** ●●●●● · **Est.** 120 min · **Lessons**
  L21.1
- **Goal:** Plan and fly KLVK → KMRY in IMC: briefing, alternate decision, fuel,
  clearance, departure, enroute, approach; divert to KSNS if you go missed.
- **Setup:** KLVK · `IFR_RAMP` · variants: KMRY above minimums (land), KMRY below
  minimums (miss, then KSNS) · `LOAD_FULL` · legs: Planning, Departure, Enroute, Approach,
  Alternate (conditional).
- **Planning fields (auto):** alternate required (yes/no by the 1-2-3 rule for the
  variant's TAF), alternate minimums met at KSNS, IFR fuel required (±1 gal Gold).
- **Criteria:** planning (auto, req, W3); clearance and departure (tiered, req, W2);
  enroute tracking and altitudes (tiered, req, W2); approach to Gold IFR standards
  (tiered, req, W3); DA decision (decision, req, W3); alternate leg flown if required
  (binary, req, W2); fuel at landing ≥ 45-min reserve (binary, req, W2); logbook complete
  (binary, W1).

#### C21.3 — Lost comms in IMC (P1)

- **Type** instrument · **Tags** failure · **Est.** 60 min · **Lessons** L21.2 ·
  **Setup:** C21.2 route · `failure` event "Two-way radio failure" during the enroute leg.
  **Goal:** squawk 7600 and fly the 91.185 route and altitudes to the approach.
  **Criteria:** 7600 (binary, req), correct route by AVEF (auto quick-pick + binary,
  req), correct altitude per segment (tiered, req), approach flown (tiered, req).

### 16.4 Challenge authoring checklist additions

v1 Section 15.4 applies. Also:

- [ ] Every variant has been flown at least once (and each decision variant both ways).
- [ ] Every `failure` event states the instructor-style action and, if offered, the sim
      menu path with a verification date.
- [ ] Auto criteria have answers and tolerances for **every** variant, checked with the
      widget that the learner will use.
- [ ] Night challenges use a time preset with `night: true` and were flown at that time.
- [ ] IFR challenges name the procedure, the d-TPP cycle, and the sim nav data cycle.
- [ ] Self-voiced scripts exist for every communication step.
- [ ] Logbook fields match what the flight actually contains.

## 17. Interactive widgets (carry-over and W21–W39)

v1 Section 16.1's shared requirements apply to every widget (SVG, mouse/touch/keyboard,
ARIA, text alternative, reduced motion, 320–1,440 px, explore/quiz modes, pure `model.ts`,
lazy loading, honest "Simplified model" labels). v2 adds:

- **Planning mode:** widgets used in planning challenges (W21, W22, W23, W20, W12) can
  emit `planningAnswer` events that fill the challenge's planning fields.
- **Drill mode:** widgets used in drills (W32, W37, W34, W19) emit scored results that the
  challenge and the practice hub record.
- **Print view:** tool widgets render a clean, black-on-white print layout for the
  kneeboard.

### 17.1 v1 P1 widgets now P0 (built in Phase 15)

W8 Airport Signs & Markings, W13 Crosswind Component, W15 Glide Range Ring, W17 METAR
Decoder (**extended with a TAF mode** for L11.2), W18 Landing Sight Picture (**extended
with a night mode** for L12.3), W19 Phonetic Alphabet Trainer, W20 Nav Log Calculator
(**extended with a fuel reserve row and night flag**). Specs as v1 Sections 16.9–16.21.

### 17.2 W21 — Density Altitude Calculator (P0) · L9.1, L13.2, L13.3, Tools

- **Shows:** inputs (field elevation, altimeter setting, OAT) → pressure altitude, ISA
  temperature, density altitude; a thermometer and altitude "ladder" graphic showing how
  high the airplane "feels".
- **Interactions:** sliders and number inputs; airport picker fills elevation from the
  region data; "Today's METAR" button (P1, Section 29.7).
- **Model:** Appendix F.1; unit tests against the Koch chart and E6B examples (⚠ store 5
  reference cases).
- **Est. build:** 1–2 days.

### 17.3 W22 — Performance Chart Reader (P0) · L9.2, L10.4, L13.3, C9.1, C10.4

- **Shows:** a POH-style table built from our sim data (D2-11) with a crosshair that
  highlights the rows and columns used; an interpolation panel showing each step; the
  corrections (wind, surface) as separate lines; a runway bar comparing required vs
  available distance.
- **Interactions:** pick table (takeoff, landing, climb, cruise); enter PA, OAT, weight,
  wind; step-through mode for learning interpolation.
- **Model:** bilinear interpolation (Appendix F.2); corrections from `aircraft.yaml`.
- **Honesty:** header "Learn-To-Fly sim data · provisional until verified" (as W5).
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.4 W23 — Weight & Balance Loader (P0) · L9.3, C9.2, Tools, Kneeboard

- **Shows:** top view of the cabin with seats, baggage areas and tanks; a loading table
  (weight, arm, moment); the CG envelope graph with the loaded point and the fuel-burn
  line from takeoff to landing.
- **Interactions:** drag passenger and bag tokens or type weights; fuel slider; "burn
  fuel" animation; warnings for max weight, compartment limits and envelope exits.
- **Model:** Appendix F.3; envelope from `aircraft.yaml`.
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.5 W24 — Mixture & EGT Trainer (P1) · L9.4

- **Shows:** EGT, fuel flow, RPM/TAS gauges; a peak-EGT curve with rich/lean-of-peak zones.
- **Interactions:** mixture lever; "find peak" quiz.
- **Model:** simplified curve from Week 1 test data; labelled "Simplified model".
- **Est. build:** 2 days.

### 17.6 W25 — Crosswind Technique & Slip Visualiser (P0) · L10.1, L10.2

- **Shows:** top view and front (pilot's-eye) view of an airplane on final with a wind
  arrow: **crab**, **wing-low (side slip)** and **forward slip** modes; ground track vs
  heading; control positions (yoke and rudder icons).
- **Interactions:** wind direction/speed; bank and rudder sliders; the ground track turns
  red when it drifts off the centreline; "touchdown" button shows side load if the
  longitudinal axis isn't aligned.
- **Model:** simple kinematics (heading, bank, drift); not a flight model.
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.7 W26 — Fronts & Clouds Cross-section (P1) · L11.1

- **Shows:** side view of cold and warm fronts with cloud types, precipitation and
  temperatures; toggle stable/unstable air.
- **Est. build:** 2–3 days.

### 17.8 W27 — Airport Lighting Explorer (P0) · L12.2, L12.3, C12.2

- **Shows:** a night-view runway and taxiway layout with edge, threshold, end, REIL,
  approach lights, PAPI and VASI, the rotating beacon, and taxiway lights.
- **Interactions:** click any light for its meaning and colour; toggle day/night; PCL
  panel ("click the mic" 3/5/7 times within 5 s → light intensity changes); PAPI/VASI
  glide path slider.
- **Accessibility:** every light has a text label; colours are never the only cue.
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.9 W28 — Night Vision & Illusions (P1) · L12.1

- **Shows:** eye diagram (rods/cones), a dark-adaptation timer, off-centre viewing demo,
  and a side-view black-hole approach showing the perceived vs actual glide path.
- **Est. build:** 2 days.

### 17.10 W29 — Mountain Airflow (P1) · L13.1

- **Shows:** a ridge cross-section with animated streamlines; windward lift, lee sink,
  rotor and wave; an aircraft icon approaching at a chosen altitude and angle.
- **Interactions:** wind speed slider; stability toggle; "cross here" button shows the
  expected up/down drafts.
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.11 W30 — Electrical System Schematic (P0) · L14.1, C14.1

- **Shows:** a simplified, original schematic of the 172S system (battery, alternator,
  buses, avionics, standby battery), with the EIS voltage and ammeter readouts.
- **Interactions:** switch BAT/ALT/avionics; fail the alternator; watch the ammeter,
  voltage and "LOW VOLTS"; shed loads and see the discharge rate and a battery-time
  estimate.
- **Model:** simple current budget from `aircraft.yaml` (⚠ numbers from Week 6).
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.12 W31 — Pitot-Static System (P0) · L14.2, C14.3

- **Shows:** the pitot tube, drain hole, static port, alternate static, and the ASI,
  altimeter and VSI.
- **Interactions:** block pitot (ram only / ram and drain), block static, open alternate
  static; then climb or descend with a slider and watch each instrument respond.
- **Model:** Appendix F.8 truth table.
- **Quiz mode:** "What will the airspeed show if you climb now?"
- **Est. build:** 2–3 days.

### 17.13 W32 — Radio Readback Trainer (P0) · L15.4, L18.3, C15.3, C18.1, Practice

- **Shows:** an ATC transmission (text + optional speech synthesis via the Web Speech
  API, with voice/speed options), an input for the learner's readback, and feedback that
  highlights each required element (callsign, runway, altitude, heading, squawk,
  frequency, hold short, clearance limit, route).
- **Modes:** VFR ground, VFR airborne, Class B/C, IFR clearance (CRAFT pad with shorthand
  boxes).
- **Model:** a tolerant parser in `shared/radio/` that normalises numbers ("one two
  thousand five hundred" → 12,500, "niner" → 9) and checks required elements per
  instruction type; unit tests with many phrasings.
- **Content:** instructions come from `content/radio-drills.yaml` (our own paraphrased
  examples; no recorded ATC audio).
- **Accessibility:** transcript always visible (speech is optional).
- **Est. build:** 4 days.

### 17.14 W33 — Instrument Scan Trainer (P0) · L17.1, L17.2

- **Shows:** a simplified PFD (original drawing) plus standby instruments; a moving
  "eye" that demonstrates a radial scan.
- **Interactions:** "What's wrong?" quiz — the widget shows a small deviation (e.g. slowly
  descending with wings level) and the learner clicks the instrument that shows it first
  and picks the correction.
- **Est. build:** 3 days.

### 17.15 W34 — Unusual Attitude Trainer (P0) · L17.3, C17.3, Practice

- **Shows:** attitude, airspeed, altimeter and turn indicators frozen in an unusual
  attitude.
- **Interactions:** the learner selects the recovery actions in order (keyboard shortcuts
  for speed); timer; feedback with the correct order.
- **Model:** attitude generator with nose-high/nose-low, left/right, and partial-panel
  variants.
- **Est. build:** 2 days.

### 17.16 W35 — Magnetic Compass Errors (P1) · L17.4

- **Shows:** a compass card that lags/leads in turns and swings with acceleration on
  east/west headings.
- **Interactions:** heading, bank and latitude sliders; "roll out on north" quiz.
- **Model:** simplified (Appendix F.7).
- **Est. build:** 2 days.

### 17.17 W36 — HSI, CDI Scaling and ILS (P0) · L18.4, L20.2–L20.4, C20.x

- **Shows:** plan and profile views of a final approach with a localizer/LPV course and
  glidepath; the G1000 HSI with the CDI and glideslope/glidepath indicator; a CDI-source
  switch (GPS/LOC/VOR) and GPS scaling (ENR/TERM/APPR).
- **Interactions:** drag the aircraft in plan and profile views; the needles respond with
  angular (ILS/LPV) or linear (GPS ENR/TERM) sensitivity; "fly" mode with wind.
- **Model:** Appendix F.6 (angular vs linear deflection, full-scale values ⚠ verify).
- **Quiz mode:** "Where is the aircraft?" and "Which way do you correct?"
- **Est. build:** 4 days.

### 17.18 W37 — Holding Pattern Entry Trainer (P0) · L19.1, L19.2, C19.1, Practice

- **Shows:** a holding fix with the inbound course, turn direction and the three entry
  sectors (70° line); an aircraft arrow; the HSI view of the same situation.
- **Interactions:** set holding course, turns (right/left), aircraft heading; the entry
  is highlighted and animated; wind option shows outbound correction and timing; quiz
  mode generates random clearances ("Which entry?") — feeds the `holding-entries` deck.
- **Model:** Appendix F.5 sector formula with boundary unit tests.
- **Est. build:** 3–4 days.

### 17.19 W38 — Approach Plate Explorer (P0) · L18.2, L20.1, L20.5, L20.6

- **Shows:** an **original, fictional "Learn-To-Fly Training Plate"** (D2-10) for an
  invented airport, laid out like an FAA plate: pilot briefing strip, plan view, profile,
  minima, airport sketch. Watermark: "Training plate — not a real procedure — not for
  navigation".
- **Interactions:** hover/tap regions and symbols for explanations; "Brief it" mode walks
  through the briefing items in order; "Fly it" mode animates the approach (vectors,
  segments, FAF, DA, missed approach) along plan and profile views together.
- **Data:** the plate is SVG + a hotspot JSON in `content/approach-plates/`. A second
  training plate (non-precision) is P1.
- **Links:** "Now open a real plate" links to the current d-TPP for each lesson example.
- **Est. build:** 5 days (most time is the drawing).

### 17.20 W39 — Descent & VDP Planner (P1) · L20.2, L20.4, Tools

- **Shows:** profile view of a non-precision final with step-downs; CDFA line vs
  dive-and-drive; VDP marker; descent rate for the groundspeed.
- **Model:** Appendix F.4.
- **Est. build:** 2 days.

## 18. Quizzes, practice and spaced repetition

### 18.1 Question types (v2 additions to v1 Section 17.1)

| Type       | Description                                                                 | Example                                             |
| ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `numeric`  | (v1) now with optional `units` shown next to the input                      | "Density altitude? (ft)"                            |
| `scenario` | A short situation with 2–3 linked `single` steps; feedback after each step  | "Cloud ahead. What now?" → "Which way do you turn?" |
| `widget`   | (v1 `hotspot` generalised) any widget quiz target (W37 entry, W34 recovery) | "Which entry?"                                      |

### 18.2 The practice hub

- **Decks** (Section 14.4) live in `content/flashcards/<deck>.yaml`. Each card has an id,
  type, front, back, explanation, `sourceLessonSlug` and optional tags.
- **Card types:** `basic` (front/back), `cloze` ("Vy is {{c1::74}} KIAS" — uses aircraft
  tokens so numbers never drift), `choice` (multiple choice), `widget` (a widget quiz item,
  e.g. a random W37 hold).
- **Unlocking:** a card enters a learner's queue when they complete its source lesson (or
  when they add the deck manually). Visitors can practise any deck without saving.
- **Session:** "Practise now" shows up to 20 due cards (new cards capped at 10/day, user
  setting). The learner rates each: Again, Hard, Good, Easy.
- **Drills:** 5-minute timed sessions built on widgets: phonetic (W19), readbacks (W32),
  holding entries (W37), unusual attitudes (W34), METAR decoding (W17).

### 18.3 Scheduling (D2-13)

`shared/practice.ts` implements an SM-2-style scheduler:

```
new card:      interval 0 → first review today
Again:         interval = 1 day, ease −0.20 (min 1.3), lapses + 1
Hard:          interval = max(1, interval × 1.2), ease −0.15
Good:          interval = first review 1 d → 3 d → interval × ease
Easy:          interval = (Good interval) × 1.3, ease +0.15
due date       = today (learner's time zone) + interval
```

Unit tests cover every branch, the ease floor, time-zone day boundaries and a 365-day
maximum interval.

### 18.4 The logbook and currency

- **Entry fields:** date, aircraft (variant), route (from/to/via), flight time (h:mm),
  day/night time, cross-country flag, simulated instrument time, approaches (count and
  type list), holds, day and night landings (full stop), remarks, `source` (`challenge` |
  `manual`), `attemptId` (if any), `estimated` (true for backfilled entries).
- **Auto entries:** created when a challenge attempt is submitted with a flight (not for
  `planning` or `drill` types), using the challenge's `logbook` fields pre-filled in the
  debrief (Section 16.1).
- **Totals:** total time, night, simulated instrument, cross-country, landings (day/night),
  approaches by type, holds; last 30/90/365 days.
- **Currency panel** (habit tracker, D2-12), computed in `shared/currency.ts`:
  - Day passenger-style: 3 takeoffs and landings in 90 days.
  - Night: 3 night full-stop landings in 90 days.
  - Instrument: 6 approaches, holding, and intercepting/tracking in the last 6 calendar
    months.
  - Each panel shows "current until <date>" or "what you need", plus a one-click link to a
    challenge that fixes it (e.g. C12.1 for night).
- **Export:** CSV (UTF-8, one row per entry) and inclusion in the full account export.

### 18.5 Behaviour and privacy

- Practice ratings and logbook entries are private to the learner; aggregated,
  anonymous stats only (Section 32.2).
- Deleting an attempt is not possible (as v1); deleting a logbook entry is (manual
  entries only; challenge entries can be edited, not deleted, to keep attempts
  consistent).

---

## 19. Content style guide additions

v1 Section 18 applies in full. Additions:

### 19.1 Terminology

- IFR phrasing exactly as the AIM/P-CG: "cleared for the ILS Runway 25 Right approach",
  "maintain 4,000 until established", "decision altitude" (DA), "minimum descent
  altitude" (MDA), "final approach fix" (FAF), "missed approach point" (MAP).
- "Simulated instrument conditions", "view-limiting device" — never "under the hood"
  alone without explaining it.
- Name approaches by their charted title ("RNAV (GPS) RWY 25R").
- Night: "night" means the logging definition unless the lesson says "sunset".
- Mountain: say "density altitude", never just "altitude", when performance is meant.

### 19.2 New callout types

| Callout            | Directive                       | Use for                                                        |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Decision           | `:::callout{type="decision"}`   | A go/no-go or divert moment (Section 13.3)                     |
| Memory item        | `:::callout{type="memory"}`     | Emergency memory items; auto-linked to the `memory-items` deck |
| In a real airplane | `:::callout{type="real-world"}` | Extra training a real pilot needs (night, mountain, IFR)       |

### 19.3 Charts and procedures in content

- Every real procedure mentioned names the **d-TPP cycle** it was checked against and
  carries "Not for navigation".
- Plan and profile diagrams in lessons are original drawings (or W38), never plate crops.
- IFR lesson footers add: "Instrument procedures change every 28 days. Never use this
  content for real IFR flight."

### 19.4 Flashcard writing rules

- One fact per card. The front asks one question; the back answers in ≤ 25 words.
- Use tokens for aircraft numbers (`{{vspeed.vy}}`), so cards update with `aircraft.yaml`.
- Every card links to its source lesson section.
- No trick cards; explanations for every `choice` card.

### 19.5 Failure and event card wording

- Start with "Simulated:" and state the failure in plain words, then the exact action to
  simulate it: "Simulated: alternator failure. Switch the ALT half of the master switch
  OFF now."
- Never tell the learner what to do next — that is what they are being tested on.

### 19.6 Content review checklist additions

- [ ] Decision points are marked with a Decision callout.
- [ ] Night, mountain and IFR lessons have an "In a real airplane" callout.
- [ ] Every rule cites its regulation/AIM section in the author notes and links to it.
- [ ] Flashcards added for every memory item and limit in the lesson.
- [ ] Procedure names and cycles recorded; "Not for navigation" present.

## 20. Information architecture and sitemap (v2)

### 20.1 Sitemap

**changed**.

```

/tools/crosswind                    W13
/tools/wind-triangle                W12
/tools/nav-log                      W20
/tools/density-altitude             W21 (new)
/tools/performance                  W22 (new)
/tools/weight-balance               W23 (new)

### 20.2 Global navigation (changes)

- **Header:** Learn (menu: Courses, Curriculum map), Challenges, **Practice** (with a due
  count badge when signed in), Reference, Tools; avatar menu adds **Logbook**.
- **Mobile drawer:** same links; Practice due count visible.
- **Footer:** adds "What's new" (P1) and a short IFR disclaimer line on IFR pages.

### 20.3 Key user flows (new)

**Flow E — From Course 1 to Course 2 (US2-01, US2-02)**

1. Course 1 complete → dashboard shows the badge and "Next: Course 2 — Advanced VFR".
2. `/courses/skyhawk-advanced-vfr` → overview, what you'll learn, modules, est. time →
   "Start Module 9".

**Flow F — Planning challenge with auto-graded answers (US2-05)**

1. `/challenges/c9-2-plan-the-load` → Brief → "Roll a scenario" → variant B.
2. **Plan** tab: W23 embedded; answers fill the planning fields; "Check my numbers"
   shows live auto results (client preview using `shared/`).
3. Fly tab → Debrief → submit → server recomputes auto criteria → result.

**Flow G — Failure during a flight (US2-04)**

1. C14.1 Fly tab → timer running → at a random time: sound + full-screen event card
   "Simulated: alternator failure. Switch ALT off now." → "Acknowledge".
2. Card time is saved; debrief shows "Failure at 12:41 — recognised at…" (learner enters).

**Flow H — Logbook and currency (US2-06–US2-08)**

1. Debrief shows a **Logbook** section pre-filled (flight time, night, landings,
   approaches) → learner corrects → submit.
2. `/logbook` shows the new row; the currency panel updates ("Night current until…").

**Flow I — Daily practice (US2-09)**

1. Dashboard card "12 cards due" → `/practice/session` → rate cards → summary ("Next
   review: tomorrow, 8 cards").

## 21. Page-by-page specifications (new and changed pages)

Format as v1 Section 20: purpose, content, states and AC.

### 21.1 Landing page `/` (changed)

- Curriculum preview becomes **three course cards** with module counts and time.
- "What's new in v2" strip (night, mountains, instrument approaches) with a live widget
  demo (W37 or W27).
- Honest scope line: "Three courses in the Cessna 172. The A380 is still the destination."
- **AC:** v1 landing AC still met (Lighthouse, CTA above the fold, no layout shift).

### 21.2 Course catalog `/courses` (new)

- Three course cards: number, title, summary, modules, lessons, challenges, est. time,
  recommended prerequisite, progress ring and badge state (signed in).
- **AC:** keyboard navigable; progress matches dashboard.

### 21.3 Course overview `/courses/:courseSlug` (new)

- Hero with course title and "What you'll be able to do"; module list with progress;
  stage check card (locked-looking but startable, with the gate warning); badge preview;
  "In a real airplane" note for Courses 2–3.
- **States:** loading, 404, error.
- **AC:** "Start/Continue course" goes to the first incomplete lesson.

### 21.4 Curriculum map `/learn` (changed)

- Course tabs (Course 1/2/3) with the v1 "flight path" per course. Remembers the last
  tab (localStorage, v1 Section 31.7 rules).

### 21.5 Lesson player (changed)

- New callouts (Decision, Memory item, In a real airplane).
- A **"Practise this"** strip at the end: the lesson's flashcards (first 3 inline, "Add
  deck to practice").
- Right rail adds "Region" (airport cards used in the lesson) for Course 2–3 lessons.

### 21.6 Challenge page (changed)

- **Brief:** "Roll a scenario" / variant picker (shows variants passed ✓); region badge;
  comms mode selector (sim ATC / network / self-voiced) where relevant; procedure name
  and d-TPP cycle + "Open current plate" link for IFR; "Print kneeboard" button.
- **Plan tab** (new, only for challenges with planning fields): embedded widgets, fields
  with units, "Check my numbers" (auto preview).
- **Fly tab:** current leg, events (cards with sound and vibration where supported),
  stopwatch with leg times, self-voiced script panel, key numbers including the planning
  results.
- **Debrief:** decision criteria first; criteria grouped by leg; auto criteria read-only
  with their computed tiers; **Logbook** section; failure timestamps shown.
- **Result:** variant shown; for packs (C10.1), pack progress; stage checks show course
  completion if achieved.
- **AC:** US2-03–US2-06; submit blocked until required non-auto criteria answered; auto
  criteria recomputed server-side; the debrief draft (sessionStorage) includes planning
  answers and logbook fields.

### 21.7 Fly mode (changed)

- Event cards full screen, dismissable with one tap or Space; legs; night theme (P1:
  dim red palette, Section 22.1); screen wake lock (P0 now).

### 21.8 Kneeboard `/kneeboard/:challengeSlug` (new)

- Print-optimised page (A5/half-letter and Letter/A4 options): brief summary, setup,
  procedure, key numbers, planning sheet (nav log W20, W&B W23, performance W22), IFR
  approach briefing card (Appendix B.12 fields), notes lines.
- **AC:** prints on one or two pages; black-on-white; no interactive chrome; readable at
  arm's length.

### 21.9 Practice pages (new)

- **`/practice`:** due today, new today, streak (days practised in a row — P1, no
  guilt messaging), decks with progress, drills.
- **`/practice/session`:** one card at a time; large type; keyboard shortcuts (Space to
  flip, 1–4 to rate); progress; summary at the end.
- **`/practice/:deckSlug`:** browse cards, add/remove deck, reset deck.
- **AC:** US2-09, US2-10; all actions keyboard-operable; visitors can practise without
  saving.

### 21.10 Logbook pages (new)

- **`/logbook`:** totals row; currency panel; table (date, aircraft, route, time, night,
  inst., approaches, landings, source); filters (date range, source); "Add flight";
  "Export CSV"; "Sim flights don't count toward real currency" banner.
- **`/logbook/new` and `/logbook/:id`:** form with validation (time h:mm, counts ≥ 0,
  night ≤ total).
- **AC:** US2-06–US2-08, US2-19; 1,000 entries paginate smoothly (50/page).

### 21.11 Reference additions (new)

- **Regions:** region page with a simplified original map (SVG), airport list, hazards,
  landmarks, charts links, challenges in the region.
- **Airports (changed):** lighting (PCL, PAPI/VASI, REIL), procedures list with d-TPP link
  and verified cycle, region badge, P1 live METAR box.
- **Weather:** scenario library (the C11.1 scenarios after they are attempted, plus
  extra examples) and W17 decoder.
- **Plates:** W38 and a list of real procedures used in challenges with links.
- **Emergencies:** memory items and flows (Appendix B) in big type, linked to the deck.

### 21.12 Dashboard (changed)

1. Course switcher (defaults to the course with the most recent activity).
2. Continue card (as v1).
3. Course progress (modules), stage check status.
4. **Practice** card (due count, "Practise now").
5. **Currency** mini-panel (three lights: current / expiring soon / not current).
6. **Logbook** totals (last 90 days).
7. Recent attempts (as v1) with variant names.
8. Refresher suggestion (v1 P1 → P0): if a skill hasn't been flown in 30 days, suggest a
   short challenge.

- **AC:** still one API call (`GET /me/dashboard`), p95 < 300 ms.

### 21.13 Account (changed)

- Preferences: cockpit variant now has an effect (Section 31.3); new cards per day;
  practice reminders on dashboard only (no email); home region.
- Security: email verification status (P1).
- Data: export now includes logbook, practice and planning data; "Export logbook CSV".

### 21.14 Admin (new, P1)

- `/admin/stats`: learning signals (v1 Section 4.3 + Section 4.3 here) with simple tables.
- `/admin/verification`: all drafts, `verify` callouts left, items with
  `lastVerifiedAt` older than 90 days, items whose `simVersion` is older than the current
  sim version, procedures whose d-TPP cycle is older than two cycles.

## 23. Accessibility and responsive additions

WCAG 2.2 AA remains the target for all P0 pages and widgets (v1 Section 22).

- **Event cards:** `role="alertdialog"`, announced by screen readers, never auto-dismiss,
  a visible text description in addition to sound; vibration is optional.
- **Timed drills:** a "no time limit" option (WCAG 2.2.1 Timing Adjustable).
- **Speech synthesis:** always optional; the transcript is always shown.
- **Flashcards:** flip is a button; content of both sides is reachable by screen readers
  after flipping; ratings have text labels and shortcuts.
- **Night theme (P1):** must keep AA contrast; it is a theme option, never forced.
- **Print:** kneeboard pages have real text (not images) and logical reading order.
- **Tables:** logbook table stacks into cards under 640 px (v1 `Table` behaviour).
- **Plan view/plates:** W38 has a full text walk-through ("Describe this plate").
- **Responsive:** Practice session and Fly mode are designed phone-first; the kneeboard
  is designed for print first and screen second.

# Part V — Technical design

## 24. Architecture changes

The architecture stays the same (v1 Section 24): one Render web service serving the API
and the SPA, MongoDB Atlas, content seeded from the repo. v2 adds:

```
                    ┌────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  Browser           │  React SPA — adds: practice, logbook,      │
                    │  kneeboard (print), courses, regions,      │
                    │  planning tab, event engine, 20 widgets    │
                    └───────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                                    │ same origin /api/v1 (additive)
                    ┌───────────────▼────────────────────────────┐
  Render web        │  Express 5 — adds: courses, regions,       │
  service           │  practice, logbook, currency, planning     │
                    │  auto-grading, password reset, admin,      │
                    │  weather proxy (P1)                        │
                    └───────┬───────────────────────┬────────────┘
                            │                       │ HTTPS (server → provider)
                    ┌───────▼────────────┐   ┌──────▼─────────────────────┐
  MongoDB Atlas     │ + courses, regions,│   │ Email provider (Resend/     │
                    │ flashcards,        │   │ Postmark) — reset & verify  │
                    │ weatherScenarios,  │   ├────────────────────────────┤
                    │ reviewStates,      │   │ aviationweather.gov (P1)    │
                    │ logbookEntries,    │   │ cached METAR/TAF            │
                    │ authTokens,        │   └────────────────────────────┘
                    │ migrations         │
                    └────────────────────┘
```

### 24.1 Request lifecycle example: submit a planning + flight attempt

1. `POST /api/v1/challenges/c9-2-plan-the-load/attempts` with `variantId`,
   `planningAnswers`, `criteriaResults` (non-auto), `logbook`, `eventsFired` and so on.
2. Middleware as v1; Zod `AttemptCreateSchema` v2 (all new fields optional).
3. `attemptService.create`:
   - loads the challenge and the variant; rejects unknown `variantId`;
   - computes auto criteria from `planningAnswers` with `shared/grading.ts`;
   - merges with learner-selected criteria; computes the score with `shared/scoring.ts`;
   - inserts the attempt; updates `challengeProgress` (best result, variants passed);
   - if the challenge is a flight, creates a `logbookEntries` document in the same
     transaction (Section 36.2) — or, without transactions, inserts the attempt first and
     the logbook entry second with a repair job (Section 36.2).
4. Returns `{ attempt, progress, logbookEntry }`; the client invalidates `dashboard`,
   `logbook`, `currency` and `course-progress` queries.

Only additions are shown (v1 Section 25 is unchanged otherwise).

```
Learn-To-Fly/

├─ server/src/

│  ├─ services/

│  │  ├─ logbook.service.ts
│  │  ├─ practice.service.ts

│  │  └─ weather.service.ts         # (P1) METAR proxy + cache
│  └─ routes/ controllers/          # courses, regions, practice, logbook, admin, weather
├─ shared/
│  ├─ grading.ts                    # auto criteria (planning/drill answers → tiers)
│  ├─ practice.ts                   # SM-2-style scheduler (D2-13)
│  ├─ currency.ts                   # day/night/instrument currency rules
│  ├─ radio/                        # readback parser (W32)
│  └─ aviation/                     # + density altitude, W&B, interpolation, holding
│                                   #   entry, ILS/CDI geometry, pitot-static table
└─ src/features/
   ├─ courses/
   ├─ practice/
   ├─ logbook/
   ├─ kneeboard/
   ├─ regions/
   ├─ admin/
   └─ widgets/                      # W21–W39 folders

## 26. Tech stack additions

| Package / service              | Purpose                                                  | Notes                                                |
| ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `resend` (or `postmark`)       | Transactional email (D2-15)                              | Server only; mocked in tests                         |
| `date-fns` + `date-fns-tz`     | Calendar-month and time-zone maths for currency/practice | Pure functions in `shared/`; keep tree-shaken        |
| `papaparse` (or hand-rolled)   | CSV export of the logbook                                | Server-side generation; escape formulas (Section 32) |
| Web Speech API (browser)       | Speech synthesis for W32 and `atc` events                | No package; feature-detect                           |
| Screen Wake Lock API (browser) | Fly mode stays awake (v1 P1 → P0)                        | Feature-detect                                       |
| `workbox-*` (P1)               | Offline fly mode                                         | Only if the service worker is built                  |
| `qrcode` (P1)                  | QR code to open fly mode on a phone                      | Client-side generation                               |

No new runtime infrastructure (no queues, no object storage). Keep the single
`package.json` (v1 Section 25.1); revisit only if the SimConnect companion starts.

---

## 27. Data model changes

Content collections are still written only by the seed script. All new fields on
existing collections are optional with defaults, so v1 documents stay valid.

### 27.1 `courses` (new)

| Field                                                     | Type                   | Notes                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `slug`, `code`, `order`                                   | string, string, number | `skyhawk-advanced-vfr`, "Course 2", 2 |
| `title`, `summary`, `description`                         | string                 |                                       |
| `moduleSlugs`                                             | string[]               | ordered                               |
| `stageCheckSlug`                                          | string                 | challenge slug                        |
| `stageCheckMinTier`                                       | enum                   | `silver` for v2 courses               |
| `badge`                                                   | object                 | `{ title, description, icon }`        |
| `recommendedCourseSlugs`                                  | string[]               | soft prerequisites                    |
| `estimatedMinutes`, `published`, `contentHash`, `version` |                        | as modules                            |

### 27.2 `regions` (new) and `airports` (changed)

- **regions:** `slug`, `name`, `summary`, `bounds` {n, s, e, w}, `charts[]`, `hazards[]`,
  `landmarks[]` {name, description, lat, lon}, `navaidIds[]`, `published`, `verifiedAt`.
- **airports** (changed): + `regionSlug`, `lighting` {beacon, pcl, papi, vasi, reil,
  approachLights}, `procedures[]` {name, type (`ils`|`loc`|`rnav-lpv`|`rnav-lnav`|`vor`|
  `circling`), dtppCycleVerified, simNavCycleVerified, notes}, `hotSpots[]`.

### 27.3 `modules`, `lessons` (changed)

- **modules:** + `courseSlug` (required after migration; seed sets it).
- **lessons:** + `flashcardDeckSlugs[]`, `regionSlugs[]`; block union + `flashcards`
  (inline deck strip), `plate` (W38 embed), `scenario` (weather scenario card).

### 27.4 `challenges` (changed)

| New field            | Type                                                                                                          | Notes                                                      |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| `regionSlug`         | string                                                                                                        | required for new challenges; v1 ones default `bay-area`    |
| `setup.timePreset`   | string                                                                                                        | `TIME_*` preset (Section 16.1); v1 `timeLocal` still works |
| `tags`               | string[]                                                                                                      | `night`, `ifr`, `mountain`, `failure`, `weather`, …        |
| `variants`           | `{ id, label, setupOverrides, tierOverrides?, planningAnswers?, weatherScenarioSlug? }[]`                     | 0–6                                                        |
| `events`             | `{ id, kind, label, message, action?, simMenuPath?, minSeconds, maxSeconds, chance?, legId?, variantIds? }[]` | replaces `randomEvents` (kept as alias)                    |
| `legs`               | `{ id, title, summary }[]`                                                                                    | criteria may reference `legId`                             |
| `planningFields`     | (v1) + `units`, `widget`, `autoGradable`                                                                      |                                                            |
| `criteria[].kind`    | + `auto`, `decision`                                                                                          | auto criteria reference `planningFieldId` or `drillId`     |
| `commsModes`         | enum[]                                                                                                        | `simAtc`, `network`, `selfVoiced`                          |
| `logbook`            | `{ night, crossCountry, instrument, approaches[], holds, landings }`                                          | defaults for the debrief                                   |
| `gateChallengeSlugs` | string[]                                                                                                      | stage checks                                               |
| `procedures`         | `{ airportIcao, name, dtppCycleVerified }[]`                                                                  | IFR                                                        |
| `telemetry` (P1)     | `{ criterionId, signal, rule }[]`                                                                             | future auto-grading (D2-04); validated, not used yet       |
| `anywhere` (P1)      | boolean                                                                                                       |                                                            |

Indexes: + `{ regionSlug: 1 }`, `{ tags: 1 }`.

### 27.5 `challengeAttempts` and `challengeProgress` (changed)

- **challengeAttempts:** + `variantId`, `planningAnswers` {fieldId: value},
  `autoResults[]` {criterionId, result, detail}, `eventsFired[]` (replaces
  `randomEventsFired`, alias kept), `legTimes[]`, `commsMode`, `logbookEntryId`.
- **challengeProgress:** + `variantsPassed[]`, `bestTierByVariant` {variantId: tier}.

### 27.6 `flashcards` (new, content) and `reviewStates` (new, user)

- **flashcards:** `id` (unique, e.g. `memory-items/engine-fire-1`), `deckSlug`, `type`,
  `front`, `back`, `explanation`, `choices?`, `widget?` {name, props}, `sourceLessonSlug`,
  `sourceSectionId`, `tags[]`, `published`, `contentHash`, `version`.
- **decks:** stored in the same collection as `{ kind: 'deck' }` documents or a small
  `flashcardDecks` collection (decide in Phase 19; record in the Decision Log).
- **reviewStates:** `userId`, `cardId`, `ease`, `intervalDays`, `dueAt`, `reps`,
  `lapses`, `lastRating`, `lastReviewedAt`, `suspended`. Unique `{ userId: 1, cardId: 1 }`;
  index `{ userId: 1, dueAt: 1 }`.

### 27.7 `logbookEntries` (new)

`userId`, `date`, `aircraftVariant`, `from`, `to`, `via[]`, `totalMinutes`,
`nightMinutes`, `simInstrumentMinutes`, `crossCountry`, `landingsDay`, `landingsNight`,
`approaches[]` {type, airportIcao, procedureName}, `holds`, `interceptTrack` (bool, for
instrument currency), `remarks` (≤ 1,000), `source` (`challenge`|`manual`), `attemptId?`,
`challengeSlug?`, `estimated` (bool). Indexes `{ userId: 1, date: -1 }`,
`{ userId: 1, attemptId: 1 }` unique sparse.

### 27.8 `weatherScenarios` (new, content)

`slug`, `title`, `route` {from, to}, `issuedAt` (fictional date/time), `metars[]`,
`tafs[]`, `gfaSummary`, `windsAloft`, `airmets[]`, `pireps[]`, `correctDecision`
(`go`|`no-go`|`delay`|`go-with-changes`), `limitingFactors[]` {id, label, correct},
`explanation`, `published`.

### 27.9 `users` (changed)

Adds `preferences.homeRegion`, `preferences.newCardsPerDay` (default 10),
`preferences.timeZone` (IANA, from the browser, editable), `preferences.nightTheme` (P1),
`emailVerifiedAt` (v1 field, now used) and `badges[]` {courseSlug, awardedAt, tier}.

### 27.10 `authTokens` (new)

`userId`, `type` (`password-reset`|`email-verify`), `tokenHash` (SHA-256), `expiresAt`,
`usedAt`. TTL index on `expiresAt`. Tokens are single-use (Section 30).

### 27.11 `migrations` (new)

`id` ("003-badges-from-v1"), `appliedAt`, `durationMs`, `gitSha`, `result` (counts).

### 27.12 Storage estimate

Logbook ≈ 0.5 KB/entry, review states ≈ 0.2 KB/card. 1,000 users × (300 entries + 300
cards) ≈ 210 MB worst case, plus v1's ~200 MB. Plan the move from M0 (512 MB) to M10
(Section 35.3) before this becomes tight.

---

## 28. Content pipeline changes

### 28.1 New validation rules

- Every module has a `courseSlug` that exists; every course lists only existing modules;
  module order is unique within a course.
- Stage checks exist, are `type: stage-check`, and every gate challenge exists.
- Variants: ids unique; `setupOverrides` only use known setup keys and known presets;
  `tierOverrides` only reference existing tiered criteria; **ids, kinds, weights and
  required flags cannot be overridden**.
- Auto criteria: every variant (or the base) provides an answer and tolerance for each
  referenced planning field; units are known.
- Events: `minSeconds < maxSeconds`; `failure` events have `action`; `legId` exists.
- Night challenges use a time preset with `night: true`; `logbook.night` matches.
- IFR challenges (`tags` includes `ifr`) have `procedures[]` with `dtppCycleVerified`
  when published; published IFR content fails validation if the cycle is older than
  **two** 28-day cycles (warning in dev, error in `--strict`).
- Flashcards: unique ids; deck exists; `cloze` uses valid token syntax; source lesson
  exists.
- Weather scenarios: exactly one `correctDecision`; at least one correct limiting factor.
- Regions: every airport has a region; every challenge airport exists; navaids exist.
- Radio drills: every instruction has a type and a list of required readback elements.

### 28.2 New directives

| Directive    | Example                                                                          | Block                 |
| ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------- |
| Flashcards   | `::flashcards{deck="memory-items" cards="3"}`                                    | `flashcards`          |
| Plate        | `::plate{slug="ltf-training-ils" mode="brief"}`                                  | `plate`               |
| Scenario     | `::scenario{slug="klvk-kmry-marine-layer"}`                                      | `scenario`            |
| New callouts | `:::callout{type="decision"} … :::`                                              | `callout`             |
| New tokens   | `{{limit.demoCrosswind}}`, `{{vspeed.va2550}}`, `{{procedure.emergencyDescent}}` | replaced at seed time |

### 28.3 Scripts

| Script                 | Change                                                               |
| ---------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `content:validate`     | New rules above; region folder discovery                             |
| `content:seed`         | New collections; unpublishes removed cards/scenarios (never deletes) |
| `content:stats`        | + per-course counts, flashcards per deck, variants per challenge     |
| `content:cycles` (new) | Lists every procedure with its verified d-TPP cycle and age          |
| `db:migrate` (new)     | Runs pending migrations (Section 36)                                 |

### 28.4 Publishing workflow (changes)

Unchanged (v1 Section 28.7), except the Render pre-deploy command becomes
`npm run db:migrate && npm run content:seed`.

## 29. API additions

All under `/api/v1`, same error shape, same CSRF and session rules (v1 Section 29).

### 29.1 Content (public, cacheable)

| Method | Path                               | Description                                                          |
| ------ | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/courses`                         | Published courses with module summaries                              |
| GET    | `/courses/:slug`                   | One course with modules, stage check summary, badge                  |
| GET    | `/regions` / `/regions/:slug`      | Regions with airports and navaids                                    |
| GET    | `/modules?course=`                 | (changed) filter by course                                           |
| GET    | `/challenges?course=&region=&tag=` | (changed) new filters                                                |
| GET    | `/flashcards/decks`                | Decks with counts                                                    |
| GET    | `/flashcards/decks/:slug`          | Cards in a deck (tokens resolved)                                    |
| GET    | `/weather-scenarios/:slug`         | Scenario **without** the answer fields                               |
| GET    | `/approach-plates/:slug`           | W38 data                                                             |
| GET    | `/radio-drills?type=`              | W32 instructions with their required elements (practice, not secret) |

### 29.2 Progress, practice and logbook (auth)

| Method | Path                               | Body                                                                                          | Description                                                      |
| ------ | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/me/courses/:slug/progress`       | —                                                                                             | Module and stage-check progress for one course                   |
| POST   | `/challenges/:slug/attempts`       | (changed) + `variantId`, `planningAnswers`, `logbook`, `eventsFired`, `legTimes`, `commsMode` | Auto criteria computed server-side                               |
| POST   | `/challenges/:slug/check-planning` | `{ variantId, planningAnswers }`                                                              | Returns auto results without saving (visitors too; rate limited) |
| GET    | `/me/practice/due`                 | —                                                                                             | `{ due, new, cards[] }` for a session (max 20)                   |
| POST   | `/me/practice/reviews`             | `{ cardId, rating, reviewedAt }`                                                              | Updates `reviewStates`; returns next due date                    |
| PUT    | `/me/practice/decks/:slug`         | `{ enabled }`                                                                                 | Add/remove a deck from the queue                                 |
| GET    | `/me/logbook`                      | query: `from`, `to`, `source`, `limit`, `before`                                              | Paginated entries + totals                                       |
| POST   | `/me/logbook`                      | entry fields                                                                                  | Manual entry                                                     |
| PATCH  | `/me/logbook/:id`                  | editable fields                                                                               | Edit (challenge entries: time, counts, remarks only)             |
| DELETE | `/me/logbook/:id`                  | —                                                                                             | Manual entries only                                              |
| GET    | `/me/logbook.csv`                  | —                                                                                             | CSV export (`Content-Disposition: attachment`)                   |
| GET    | `/me/currency`                     | —                                                                                             | Day/night/instrument status + what's needed                      |
| GET    | `/me/dashboard`                    | —                                                                                             | (changed) adds course, practice, currency, logbook               |

### 29.3 Auth (v1 P1 → P0, plus verification)

| Method | Path                      | Body                     | Response                    |
| ------ | ------------------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------- |
| POST   | `/auth/forgot-password`   | `{ email }`              | 202 always (no enumeration) |
| POST   | `/auth/reset-password`    | `{ token, newPassword }` | 204; all sessions revoked   |
| POST   | `/auth/send-verification` | —                        | 202 (P1; rate limited)      |
| POST   | `/auth/verify-email`      | `{ token }`              | 204 (P1)                    |

### 29.4 Admin (role `admin`, P1)

| Method | Path                  | Description                                        |
| ------ | --------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/admin/stats`        | Learning signals (Sections 4.3 here and in v1)     |
| GET    | `/admin/verification` | Unverified/outdated content report (Section 21.14) |
| GET    | `/admin/migrations`   | Applied migrations                                 |

### 29.5 What's new (P1)

| Method | Path         | Description                                                   |
| ------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/whats-new` | Recent `contentReleases` with human-readable changes (public) |

### 29.6 Grading contract

- `shared/grading.ts` exports `gradeAuto(challenge, variantId, answers) → autoResults[]`.
  The server always recomputes; client results are previews.
- Answers are validated with Zod (numbers finite, within sane ranges, units match).
- Weather-scenario answers and planning answers are **not** sent in public content
  responses; `/check-planning` returns only tiers and explanations.

### 29.7 Live weather proxy (P1)

| Method | Path                   | Description                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------ | ---------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/weather/metar/:icao` | Server fetches the METAR/TAF from aviationweather.gov (⚠ verify API and terms), caches 10 min in memory, returns raw + decoded + `fetchedAt` + "Not for real flight" |

- `:icao` must match `^[A-Z0-9]{3,4}$` **and** exist in the regions data (no open proxy,
  no SSRF). Timeouts 3 s; failures return 503 with a friendly message.

---

## 30. Authentication and account additions

### 30.1 Password reset (US2-14)

1. `POST /auth/forgot-password` → always 202 with the same message; if the email exists,
   create a 32-byte random token, store its SHA-256 hash in `authTokens` (expires 30 min),
   and email a link `/reset-password/:token`.
2. `/reset-password/:token` → new password form (same rules as sign-up).
3. `POST /auth/reset-password` → hash the token, find an unused, unexpired record, set the
   new password, mark the token used, revoke all sessions, set `passwordChangedAt`,
   email a "your password was changed" notice.
4. Rate limits: 5 requests per hour per IP and 3 per email per hour.

### 30.2 Email verification (P1)

- Sent on sign-up and on request. Not required to use the site; a gentle banner until
  verified. Same token design (24-hour expiry).

### 30.3 Account export and deletion (changed)

- Export adds `logbookEntries`, `reviewStates`, `badges`, attempts' planning data.
- Deletion removes the same plus `authTokens`. Test that nothing remains (Section 34).

---

## 31. Frontend architecture additions

### 31.1 Routing

- New lazy routes for courses, practice, logbook, kneeboard, regions, tools, admin
  (Section 20.1). Admin routes are guarded by role on both client and server.
- `/learn/:moduleSlug` resolves the course from the module (no URL change, D2-16).

### 31.2 Challenge event engine

- `src/features/challenges/events/` — a small state machine per Fly session:
  `idle → running (leg n) → event fired → acknowledged → … → finished`.
- Random times are drawn when the learner presses Start (seeded by attempt start time so a
  refresh doesn't change them); stored in sessionStorage with the debrief draft.
- Sounds via the Web Audio API with a user gesture unlock; a visual card always appears.

### 31.3 Classic-panel mode (v1.1 carry-over)

- `preferences.cockpitVariant = 'classic'`: callouts of type `classic` are expanded and
  moved above their G1000 counterparts; image blocks with a `classic` alternative swap
  source; W2 shows a "Classic equivalent" note; IFR course pages show "Course 3 uses the
  G1000 (D2-07)".

### 31.4 Printing

- `@media print` styles in `src/styles/print.css`; `PrintLayout` hides navigation;
  widgets expose `printView` components (static SVG + tables).

### 31.5 Data fetching

- Query keys added: `['course', slug]`, `['course-progress', slug]`, `['practice-due']`,
  `['logbook', filters]`, `['currency']`, `['regions']`.
- Practice reviews use optimistic updates (the next card appears immediately); failures
  are retried and surfaced as a toast.

### 31.6 Client-side persistence (additions to v1 Section 31.7)

- Visitor practice progress (sessionStorage only; offered for import after sign-up, P1).
- Fly session state (events, leg times) in sessionStorage with the debrief draft.

---

## 32. Security and privacy additions

### 32.1 Security

- **Tokens:** random 32 bytes, stored hashed, single-use, short expiry, constant-time
  comparison; reset revokes all sessions (Section 30.1).
- **Email:** provider API key only on the server; never log email bodies or tokens;
  redact tokens from URLs in logs (pino redaction paths).
- **CSV export:** escape cells that start with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@` (CSV/formula injection).
- **Weather proxy:** allow-list of ICAO codes from our own data; fixed upstream host;
  timeouts; no user-controlled URLs (SSRF).
- **Admin:** role checked server-side on every admin route; admin creation stays a script
  (`scripts/create-admin.ts`, v1 P1 → P0).
- **Rate limits:** `/check-planning` 30/min per IP; practice reviews 120/min per user;
  logbook writes 60/min per user.
- **CSP:** allow the email provider only server-side (no client change); speech synthesis
  and wake lock need no CSP changes. Re-check after adding any new third party.

### 32.2 Privacy

- Update the privacy policy (v1 Section 57.4): the email provider as a processor; logbook
  and practice data; time zone; the optional live-weather feature (no personal data sent
  upstream).
- Emails are only for account security. No marketing, no tracking pixels.
- Learning-signal stats remain aggregated and anonymous.

---

## 33. Performance

- Budgets unchanged (v1 Section 33.1) plus Section 4.2 here.
- New features are lazy chunks: practice, logbook, kneeboard, admin, each widget.
- `/me/practice/due` uses the `{ userId, dueAt }` index and returns at most 20 cards with
  their content (one query for states, one `$in` query for cards).
- `/me/logbook` uses cursor pagination (`before`) and a cached totals aggregation per user
  (recomputed on write, stored on the user document as `logbookTotals`).
- `/me/dashboard` still one call; its new sections are small aggregates.
- Content payloads: lessons remain pre-parsed; flashcards are fetched per deck.

---

## 34. Testing strategy additions

### 34.1 Key test cases (must exist)

**Grading (`shared/grading.ts`)**

- Numeric answer exactly on a tier boundary → the better tier (document inclusivity).
- Units mismatch → validation error, not a wrong answer.
- Every variant of every auto-graded challenge has answers (content test).
- Weather scenario: correct decision + all correct factors → Gold; one wrong factor → Silver.

**Scoring (`shared/scoring.ts`)**

- Decision criterion not met and required → not passed.
- Auto criteria contribute points exactly like selected ones.
- Stage check course completion: Silver threshold enforced; v1 course grandfathering.

**Practice (`shared/practice.ts`)**

- Each rating branch; ease floor 1.3; max interval 365 days; due dates across a DST change
  and across midnight in the learner's time zone.

**Currency (`shared/currency.ts`)**

- 90-day windows (inclusive/exclusive edges documented); night landings require
  full-stop night landings; instrument currency uses **calendar months** (e.g. an approach
  on 15 March counts through 30 September).

**Aviation maths (additions)**

- Density altitude against 5 reference cases; W&B CG against a hand-worked example;
  bilinear interpolation on table edges; holding entry sectors at 70°/110°/180° boundaries
  for right and left turns; ILS deflection at 1, 5 and 10 nm; pitot-static truth table.

**Radio parser (`shared/radio/`)**

- Number normalisation (niner, tree, fife, thousand, hundred); callsign order; missing
  hold-short → required element missing; extra wrong altitude → flagged.

**API**

- Password reset: 202 for unknown email; token single-use; expired token rejected; all
  sessions revoked after reset.
- Attempt with unknown `variantId` → 400; with tampered auto results → server recomputes.
- Logbook: user A cannot read or edit user B's entries; challenge entries can't be
  deleted; CSV escapes formulas.
- Practice due/review idempotency (same review twice within a second → one update).
- Weather proxy rejects unknown ICAO codes (P1).
- Account deletion removes logbook, review states and tokens.

**Migrations**

- Each migration is idempotent (run twice → same result) and tested against a fixture DB
  that looks like v1 production.

**E2E (Playwright) additions**

1. Course catalog → Course 2 → Module 9 → L9.3 → W23 keyboard use.
2. C9.2: roll variant → plan tab → check numbers → debrief → result shows auto tiers.
3. C14.1 with a forced short event window (test-only query param) → event card appears →
   acknowledge → debrief shows the time.
4. Debrief logbook section → submit → `/logbook` shows the entry → currency updates.
5. Practice session: rate 5 cards → due count decreases → keyboard only.
6. Kneeboard print view renders (Playwright PDF snapshot size check).
7. Password reset with the email service mocked (token read from the test mailbox).
8. axe scan on every new page and on the event card dialog.

### 34.2 Test data

- Fixture content grows to include one course with a stage check, a variant challenge, an
  auto-graded challenge, one deck and one weather scenario.
- A v1-shaped fixture database for migration tests.

---

## 35. CI, environments and deployment changes

### 35.1 CI

- `content` job runs the new validation rules and `content:cycles` (warning report).
- `test` job runs the migration tests against mongodb-memory-server.
- `e2e` job gains the new flows; split into two shards if it exceeds 15 minutes.
- Bundle-size check covers the new lazy chunks (fail if a widget chunk > 80 KB gzipped;
  target 60 KB).

### 35.2 Environments and variables

New variables (Appendix E): `EMAIL_PROVIDER`, `EMAIL_API_KEY`, `EMAIL_FROM`,
`APP_BASE_URL` (for links in emails; may reuse `PUBLIC_SITE_URL`), `FEATURE_LIVE_WEATHER`
(P1), `WEATHER_API_BASE` (P1), `AUTH_TOKEN_TTL_MINUTES`.

### 35.3 Deployment

- Pre-deploy command: `npm run db:migrate && npm run content:seed`.
- Move production to **Atlas M10** (or higher) before running v2 migrations, to get
  automated backups and point-in-time restore (⚠ check current pricing; record in the
  Decision Log). Until then: `mongodump` before every migration (Section 36.4).
- DNS for the email provider: SPF, DKIM (and DMARC) records on the sending domain.
- Cost estimate additions: email provider free tier likely enough; Atlas M10 ≈ the
  largest new cost (⚠ verify).

---

## 36. Data migrations

v1 had no migration framework because content collections are re-seeded and user data
never changed shape. v2 changes user data, so it adds one.

### 36.1 Framework

- `server/src/migrations/NNN-short-name.ts` exports `{ id, description, up(db, log) }`.
  No `down`: every migration is additive and idempotent; recovery is by backup restore.
- `scripts/migrate.ts` (`npm run db:migrate`) loads migrations in order, skips applied
  ones (from the `migrations` collection), runs each with a lock document to prevent two
  deploys migrating at once, and records counts.
- `--dry-run` prints what would change (counts) without writing.

### 36.2 Consistency without multi-document transactions

- Atlas M10+ supports transactions; use them for attempt + logbook writes when available.
- Otherwise: write the attempt, then the logbook entry with `attemptId` (unique sparse
  index prevents duplicates); a startup repair job creates missing logbook entries for
  attempts from the last 24 hours that should have one.

### 36.3 Planned migrations

| ID  | Name                    | What it does                                                                                      |
| --- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 001 | `indexes-v2`            | Creates indexes for the new collections and new fields                                            |
| 002 | `users-preferences-v2`  | Sets defaults: `homeRegion: 'bay-area'`, `newCardsPerDay: 10`, `timeZone: null`, `badges: []`     |
| 003 | `badges-from-v1`        | Awards the Course 1 badge to users who completed Course 1 under v1 rules (grandfathered)          |
| 004 | `attempts-v2-fields`    | Copies `randomEventsFired` → `eventsFired`; sets `variantId: null` on existing attempts           |
| 005 | `progress-variants`     | Sets `variantsPassed: []` on `challengeProgress`                                                  |
| 006 | `logbook-backfill` (P1) | Creates `estimated: true` logbook entries from v1 flight attempts (duration = challenge estimate) |

Content moves (airports into regions, `courseSlug` on modules) need no migration: the seed
upserts them by slug/ICAO.

### 36.4 Production migration runbook

1. Announce a short maintenance window (optional; migrations are additive).
2. Take a backup (`mongodump` or an Atlas snapshot); record document counts per
   collection.
3. Run `npm run db:migrate -- --dry-run` against production; compare with the fixture run.
4. Deploy (pre-deploy runs migrations, then the seed).
5. Check the `migrations` collection, counts, `/api/v1/health` and a test account.
6. If anything is wrong: roll back the service (v1 Section 38.5) and restore the backup.

---

# Part VI — Execution plan

## 37. Phase overview and timeline

### 37.1 Assumptions

- One developer/author, ~10–12 hours per week (as v1).
- v1.0 is live and v1 Phase 13 is complete before Phase 14 starts. v1 content verification
  left over from v1 D-21 is finished first — v2 is built on verified foundations.
- **Content model:** as in v1 Phase 9 (v1 D-21), the assistant drafts lessons and
  challenges from the specs as drafts (`published: false`), and you verify each item in
  the sim, add screenshots and publish. Estimates assume ~2 hours of your time per lesson
  and ~2–3 hours per challenge. Writing everything yourself roughly doubles Phases 22–23.
- Add 20–30% buffer for life.

### 37.2 Phases

| Phase | Name                               | Depends on            | Est. (weeks)            | Parallel track                                |
| ----- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| 14    | v2 kick-off                        | v1 Phase 13           | 1 (+11 study, parallel) | Study runs through Phase 21                   |
| 15    | v1.1 carry-over                    | 14                    | 4                       | Can release as `v1.1.0`                       |
| 16    | Courses, regions and migrations    | 15                    | 2                       |                                               |
| 17    | Challenge engine v2                | 16                    | 3                       |                                               |
| 18    | Logbook, currency and dashboard v2 | 17                    | 2                       |                                               |
| 19    | Practice hub                       | 16                    | 2                       | Parallel with 17–18                           |
| 20    | VFR widgets (W21–W32)              | 15                    | 4                       | Starts alongside 16                           |
| 21    | IFR widgets (W33–W39)              | 20                    | 3.5                     |                                               |
| 22    | Content: Course 2                  | 16, 17 (+ study 1–7)  | 12 (parallel)           | Module by module; publish as verified (D2-17) |
| 23    | Content: Course 3                  | 21, 22 (+ study 8–11) | 9 (parallel)            |                                               |
| 24    | Reference, tools and kneeboard v2  | 16, 20                | 1.5                     |                                               |
| 25    | Polish, QA and in-sim verification | 18–24                 | 2.5                     |                                               |
| 26    | Release v2.0.0                     | 25                    | 1                       |                                               |

**Critical path:** 14 → 15 → 16 → 17 → 22 (Course 2 content) → 23 (Course 3 content) →
25 → 26 ≈ **34–38 weeks**. Content is the long pole (R2-05); engineering phases 18–21
and 24 fit alongside it.

### 37.3 Milestones

| Milestone            | When     | What is demonstrable                                                  |
| -------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| M2-A "v1.1"          | ~week 5  | v1 P1 content and widgets, password reset, tools hub, classic mode    |
| M2-B "Courses"       | ~week 7  | Course catalog; Course 1 as a course; regions; migrations run in prod |
| M2-C "Engine v2"     | ~week 10 | C9.2 with auto-graded planning; C14.1 with a failure card             |
| M2-D "Staying sharp" | ~week 13 | Logbook, currency panel, practice hub with the first decks            |
| M2-E "Course 2 live" | ~week 24 | All Course 2 P0 content verified and published                        |
| M2-F "Course 3 live" | ~week 32 | All Course 3 P0 content verified and published                        |
| M2-G "v2.0.0"        | ~week 36 | Release QA passed; demo ready                                         |

### 37.4 Working rhythm

Unchanged from v1 Section 39.4: plan the week from this document, one PR per step (or
small group of steps) with the step ID in the title, a phase summary with **What I
changed / What I couldn't do / What I need from you**, and `CHANGELOG.md` updated per
phase.

---

## 38. Phase 14 — v2 kick-off

**Goal:** v1 is closed out, v2 decisions are confirmed, and study and tooling for v2 are
in place.
**Est.** 1 week (+ 11 weeks of study in parallel).

### 38.1 Close out v1

- [ ] 14.1 v1 retrospective (v1 step 13.12) reviewed; update the v2 Decision Log with
      anything it changes.
- [ ] 14.2 Confirm every v1 P0 item is verified and published (v1 Section 49.4 ticked).
- [ ] 14.3 Triage v1 launch feedback into Phase 15 (small fixes) or the v2 parking lot.
- [ ] 14.4 Record the current sim version and nav-data cycle in the Decision Log.

### 38.2 Confirm decisions and accounts

- [ ] 14.5 Review D2-01…D2-17 and confirm or change each (PR "Phase 14: confirm v2
      decisions").
- [ ] 14.6 Choose the email provider (Resend or Postmark); create the account; verify the
      sending domain (SPF/DKIM) — see Appendix G "What you must do personally".
- [ ] 14.7 Decide on Atlas M10 timing (before Phase 16 migrations or at Phase 26).
- [ ] 14.8 (P1) Create a VATSIM account and install the pilot client (for L15.5 research).

### 38.3 Study and reference material

- [ ] 14.9 Download the IFH, IPH, Instrument Rating ACS, the current d-TPP pages for
      Section 12.4 airports, the IFR low chart, and "Tips on Mountain Flying" into your
      local `reference/` folder (outside the repo).
- [ ] 14.10 Start the v2 self-study path (Section 7.1).
- **AC:** decisions confirmed in the log; email domain verified; study started.
